The core UN human-rights treaty architecture remains current, but party status, declarations/reservations, optional procedures, derogations and domestic remedies vary by treaty and State. BUILD02 therefore preserves a live treaty/jurisdiction gate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **OHCHR — Status of Ratification Interactive Dashboard**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-046-HUMAN_RIGHTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "人權法"
  english_name: "Human Rights Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當個人面對國家、公權力或國家未能防止的重大私人侵害時，哪一個人權法源在該法域對該主體具有拘束力；哪一項權利被干預；國家是否有尊重、保護、實現、調查與救濟義務；以及限制、減損或差別待遇能否取得法律資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統公法常把國家視為權利來源，但二十世紀國際人權法逐步把某些基本利益放在國家權力之前，要求國家即使透過合法國內程序也不能任意剝奪生命、自由、人格、平等與其他受保護權利。人權法因此建立普遍權利、國際條約義務、監督機制、有效救濟與國家責任，並逐步發展正面義務、反歧視與經社文權的制度框架。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家主權 vs 個人國際人權"
    - "民主決定 vs 少數／個人權利"
    - "權利普遍性 vs 法域與條約拘束範圍"
    - "自由權消極義務 vs 國家積極保護義務"
    - "立即履行義務 vs 經社文權逐步實現"
    - "公共利益限制 vs 權利核心"
    - "國家安全／緊急狀態 vs 不可減損權利"
    - "私人侵害 vs 國家保護責任"
    - "國際監督 vs 國內救濟先行"
    - "象徵性權利承認 vs 可執行救濟"

  shortest_maxim:
    "人權法第一眼不是『這件事很嚴重』；先問『哪個權利、誰是權利主體、哪個國家／機關被哪個法源拘束、行為落不落在其管轄、限制有無法律資格、救濟在哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 52.1 第一眼｜RIGHT → HOLDER → DUTY-BEARER → SOURCE → JURISDICTION → INTERFERENCE → JUSTIFICATION → REMEDY

```text
RIGHT
＝生命？自由？公平審判？隱私？言論？宗教？平等？健康？教育？住房？

HOLDER
＝誰是權利主體？公民？外國人？兒童？被拘禁者？法人？

DUTY-BEARER
＝哪個國家、機關、地方政府、國營／受委託主體？

SOURCE
＝憲法？ICCPR？ICESCR？CRC？CEDAW？CAT？區域人權公約？

JURISDICTION
＝這個人／行為是否落在該國際法源的管轄範圍？

INTERFERENCE
＝國家做了什麼／沒有做什麼？

JUSTIFICATION
＝權利可否限制？法律依據、正當目的、必要性、比例？

REMEDY
＝國內法院？行政？憲法？條約機構？區域法院？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 52.18 證據特徵

- applicable treaty status；
- reservations / declarations；
- constitutional text；
- statute；
- government order；
- detention record；
- policy；
- statistics；
- budget；
- impact assessment；
- correspondence；
- investigation file；
- medical evidence；
- witness testimony；
- remedy record；
- treaty-body / regional decisions；
- implementation evidence。

核心：

> **人權案的第一份證據常不是「受害很嚴重」，而是「哪個國家權力在什麼法源與管轄下，對哪個受保護利益做了什麼」。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置、地方制度、最新法規或動態名單，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 52.20 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依國家、條約與權利現查：

1. treaty ratification / accession；
2. reservations / declarations；
3. domestic incorporation；
4. constitutional status；
5. right-holder；
6. jurisdiction / extraterritoriality；
7. scope of right；
8. limitation test；
9. derogation；
10. non-derogable obligations；
11. equality / discrimination；
12. positive obligations；
13. corporate / horizontal dimensions；
14. remedies；
15. exhaustion；
16. Optional Protocol / individual petition；
17. regional human-rights jurisdiction；
18. implementation / reparation。

The Convention on the Rights of the Child remains the baseline; General Comment No. 25 specifically addresses children’s rights in the digital environment. Treaty/Optional Protocol status and domestic child-protection implementation remain jurisdiction-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Committee on the Rights of the Child — General Comment No. 25 (2021)**

  - Verified point: General Comment No. 25 addresses children’s rights in relation to the digital environment.
- **OHCHR — Status of Ratification Interactive Dashboard**
  - URL: https://indicators.ohchr.org/
  - Verified point: Treaty participation is dynamic by instrument and country; declarations/reservations and procedures require treaty-specific checking.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:
  domain_id: "DLM-050-CHILD_RIGHTS_CHILD_PROTECTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "兒少法"
  english_name: "Child Rights / Child Protection Law"
  domain_core_question: "兒童因年齡與依賴關係需要特殊保護，但同時是獨立權利主體；法律如何在最佳利益、兒童意見、evolving capacities、父母責任、國家保護、教育、勞動、數位環境與少年司法中，避免把保護變成全面替代決定？"
  historical_reason: "傳統法律常把兒童視為父母的附屬或尚未成為完整人的對象。CRC 將兒童轉為獨立 rights-holder：有生命發展、身份、表意、隱私、家庭、教育、健康、遊戲與免受暴力剝削的權利；同時承認父母責任與兒童能力會隨年齡發展。兒少法因此不是把成人法縮小，而是建立保護、參與、發展與適齡自主並存的制度。"
  fundamental_tension:
    - "保護 vs 自主"
    - "父母權責 vs 兒童獨立權利"
    - "最佳利益 vs 兒童意見"
    - "安全介入 vs 家庭完整"
    - "程序速度 vs 正確事實認定"
    - "兒童隱私 vs safeguarding"
    - "數位參與 vs online harm"
    - "少年責任 vs rehabilitation / reintegration"
    - "年齡門檻確定性 vs evolving capacities"
  shortest_maxim: "兒少法第一眼不是『大人覺得怎樣對孩子最好』；先問『孩子是誰、幾歲、能理解多少、面臨什麼風險、孩子怎麼說、誰有責任、國家介入到什麼程度才必要』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 56.1 第一眼｜AGE / STATUS → RIGHT → RISK → CAPACITY → CHILD'S VIEW → PARENTAL RESPONSIBILITY → STATE DUTY → MEASURE → REVIEW

```text
AGE / STATUS＝未滿 18？有無特殊年齡門檻？
RIGHT＝安全？家庭？教育？健康？隱私？表意？身份？
RISK＝暴力？疏忽？性剝削？trafficking？網路？勞動？司法？
CAPACITY＝孩子對此具何理解與決策能力？
CHILD'S VIEW＝有沒有以安全、適齡方式被聽見？
PARENTAL RESPONSIBILITY＝父母／監護人依法應做什麼？
STATE DUTY＝支持家庭？保護？調查？替代照護？司法介入？
MEASURE＝是否必要、比例、最少侵入？
REVIEW＝何時複查？孩子能否申訴？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 56.20 證據特徵

- birth / age documents；child interview；school；medical；social worker；family records；police；digital records；platform reports；labour records；placement history；juvenile case file；risk assessment；complaint record。

核心：

> **兒少案件不能只蒐集成人怎麼描述孩子；也要保留孩子自己的經驗與制度實際如何作用在他身上的材料。**

```yaml

  qualified_status: "CRPD_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__EU_ACCESSIBILITY_ACT_APPLICABLE_FROM_2025_06_28_WITH_TRANSITIONS__DOMESTIC_CAPACITY_ACCOMMODATION_AND_SERVICE_SCOPE_REQUIRED"
```

CRPD status remains treaty-specific, while the EU Accessibility Act applies to covered products and services from 28 June 2025 subject to its scope and transitional rules. Accessibility does not erase domestic legal-capacity, accommodation or sector rules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN CRPD Committee — Report on 34th session (2026)**
  - URL: https://docstore.ohchr.org/
  - Verified point: The Committee reported 193 States Parties to CRPD and 108 to the Optional Protocol as at 27 March 2026; treaty participation remains dynamic.
- **EUR-Lex — Directive (EU) 2019/882 (European Accessibility Act)**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2019/882/oj
  - Verified point: Covered products/services fall within the Directive’s application from 28 June 2025, subject to scope and transitional provisions.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:
  domain_id: "DLM-051-DISABILITY_RIGHTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "身心障礙權利法"
  english_name: "Disability Rights Law"
  domain_core_question: "法律如何從『缺陷／照護對象』框架轉向『平等權利主體』框架；當環境、制度、溝通或態度與個人長期障礙相互作用時，國家與私人主體何時必須移除障礙、提供合理調整、承認法律能力與支持性決策，讓身心障礙者可以實際參與教育、工作、司法、社區與政治生活？"
  historical_reason: "過去法律常以醫療、慈善、監護與隔離方式處理身心障礙，將問題視為個人缺陷。CRPD 改變法理焦點：persons with disabilities 是 rights holders，障礙也來自 impairments 與 attitudinal / environmental barriers 的相互作用。法律因此從單純照護走向 equality、accessibility、reasonable accommodation、legal capacity、independent living、participation 與 supported decision-making。"
  fundamental_tension:
    - "保護／照護 vs autonomy / legal capacity"
    - "形式平等 vs 實質平等"
    - "醫療診斷 vs human-rights model"
    - "一般制度效率 vs reasonable accommodation"
    - "個別調整 vs system-wide accessibility"
    - "替代決策 vs supported decision-making"
    - "機構照護 vs community living"
    - "安全風險 vs dignity of risk"
    - "專業評估 vs 本人意願與偏好"
    - "資源限制 vs immediate non-discrimination duties"
  shortest_maxim: "身心障礙權利法第一眼不是『這個人哪裡有問題』；先問『哪個環境／制度造成障礙、本人要做什麼、需要什麼支持、誰拒絕了什麼調整、這個拒絕是否有法律資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 57.1 第一眼｜PERSON → IMPAIRMENT / BARRIER → ACTIVITY → RIGHT → ACCESSIBILITY → ACCOMMODATION → SUPPORT → DECISION → REMEDY

```text
PERSON＝本人是誰？不要先用診斷取代人。
IMPAIRMENT / BARRIER＝身體、感官、智能、心理社會？環境／態度／資訊障礙？
ACTIVITY＝教育？工作？交通？司法？投票？居住？醫療？
RIGHT＝哪個 CRPD／國內權利？
ACCESSIBILITY＝制度是否本來就應對所有人可近？
ACCOMMODATION＝此人是否需要特定合理調整？
SUPPORT＝溝通、personal assistance、decision support、assistive tech？
DECISION＝誰作決定？本人 wishes / preferences 如何被尊重？
REMEDY＝申訴、調整、停止歧視、賠償、司法救濟？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 57.22 證據特徵

- accommodation request；accessibility audit；job/course essential functions；medical / functional evidence；communication needs；assistive technology；cost estimates；alternatives；denial reasons；guardianship/support orders；本人 wishes / preferences record；service plan；employment/education data；consultation records。

核心：

> **身心障礙權利案件不是證明這個人「有多不正常」，而是證明哪個權利被哪個 barrier 阻斷、什麼支持可以讓他平等參與。**

---

## 57.24 Positive Law Gate

依國家、CRPD status、權利領域與個案現查：disability definition、discrimination、reasonable accommodation、accessibility、universal design、legal capacity、guardianship / supported decision-making、access to justice、liberty / involuntary treatment、freedom from abuse、community living、inclusive education、employment、health、social protection、political participation、data/confidentiality、certification/eligibility、remedies、CRPD Optional Protocol。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_EQUALITY_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__2024_EQUALITY_BODIES_TRANSPOSITION_DEADLINE_PASSED_2026_06_19__NATIONAL_IMPLEMENTATION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Directives (EU) 2024/1499 and 2024/1500 required transposition by 19 June 2026 and apply their replacement equality-body provisions from that date. Deadline passed does not equal complete or uniform national implementation.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Council Directive (EU) 2024/1499**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/1499/oj
  - Verified point: Transposition required by 19 June 2026; replacement equality-body provisions apply from that date.
- **EUR-Lex — Directive (EU) 2024/1500**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/1500/oj
  - Verified point: Transposition required by 19 June 2026; the Directive is in force and Article 23 applies from that date.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-185-EQUALITY_ANTI_DISCRIMINATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "平等／反歧視法"
  english_name: "Equality / Anti-Discrimination Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "公共機關、雇主、教育機構、商品服務提供者、金融機構、房東、專業者或其他受規範主體，何時因受保護特徵／身分、其關聯或推定特徵而給予直接或間接不利待遇；harassment、reasonable accommodation、equal pay、victimisation／retaliation、positive action、occupational requirements、objective justification、burden of proof與equality-body／法院救濟如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "形式上相同的規則可能在既有社會結構中造成不成比例排除；反過來，任何差異待遇也不能只因結果不同就被命名為歧視。現代平等法因此同時發展直接歧視、間接歧視、騷擾、合理調整、同工同酬、反報復與積極措施等不同資格鏈，並把舉證與專門 equality bodies 納入制度。"

  first_move:
    "先固定受規範 field、法定 protected ground 與 claimant status，再辨 direct / indirect / harassment / accommodation / equal-pay / retaliation 路徑；把 adverse treatment、comparator或group disadvantage、causal link、justification、exception、burden shifting與remedy分開。不得以『我覺得不公平』直接替 protected-ground nexus 完成。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "formal equality vs substantive equality"
    - "same treatment vs accommodation of difference"
    - "individual autonomy vs structural exclusion"
    - "anti-classification vs positive action"
    - "objective criteria vs disparate impact"
    - "employer / service-provider freedom vs equal access"
    - "privacy of sensitive traits vs proof of discrimination"
    - "victim protection vs respondent procedural fairness"
    - "uniform protection vs ground-specific / sector-specific scope"
    - "judicial remedy vs institutional equality enforcement"

  shortest_maxim:
    "反歧視第一眼不是『待遇不公平』；先問『在哪個法律領域、哪個受保護特徵、什麼待遇或規則、直接還是間接、差異如何連到該特徵、是否有法定正當化／例外、誰負什麼舉證與救濟責任』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-3.1 第一眼｜FIELD → PROTECTED GROUND → CONDUCT / RULE → DISADVANTAGE → NEXUS → TYPE → COMPARATOR / GROUP EFFECT → JUSTIFICATION → ACCOMMODATION → BURDEN → REMEDY

```text
FIELD
＝employment? education? goods/services? social security? housing? public power?

PROTECTED GROUND
＝race? sex? religion? disability? age? sexual orientation? other statutory ground?

CONDUCT / RULE
＝decision? criterion? practice? harassment? failure to accommodate?

DISADVANTAGE
＝what concrete detriment / barrier?

NEXUS
＝because of / linked to / disproportionately affects?

TYPE
＝direct? indirect? harassment? victimisation? equal pay? accommodation?

COMPARATOR / GROUP EFFECT
＝required? hypothetical? statistical? unnecessary under exact route?

JUSTIFICATION
＝legitimate aim? necessity? proportionality? genuine occupational requirement?

ACCOMMODATION
＝needed? reasonable? disproportionate burden?

BURDEN
＝prima facie facts? burden shift? respondent explanation?

REMEDY
＝injunction? compensation? reinstatement? equality-body process? administrative sanction?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-3.17 Evidence architecture

- decision records；
- recruitment / promotion scores；
- comparator files；
- pay data；
- workplace policies；
- accommodation request；
- medical / functional evidence where lawful；
- emails / messages；
- harassment logs；
- complaint records；
- retaliation timeline；
- statistical data；
- algorithm / model documentation；
- audit logs；
- equality-body correspondence；
- reasons / exceptions；
- occupational-requirement evidence。

核心：

> **反歧視不是替所有不公平命名；它要求把特徵、待遇、因果、結構性不利、正當化與救濟放進可證明的法律鏈。**

ILO Convention 169 remains in force for ratifying States and NORMLEX shows 24 ratifications in August 2026. UNDRIP and other international standards do not eliminate the need to verify domestic recognition, consultation/FPIC, land, resource and remedy rules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ILO NORMLEX — C169 Indigenous and Tribal Peoples Convention status**
  - URL: https://normlex.ilo.org/dyn/nrmlx_en/f?p=NORMLEXPUB:11300:0::NO::P11300_INSTRUMENT_ID:312314
  - Verified point: C169 entered into force in 1991 and NORMLEX showed 24 ratifications in August 2026.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 RIGHTS_STATUS_EQUALITY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-198-INDIGENOUS_PEOPLES_TRIBAL_RIGHTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "原住民族／部落權利法"
  english_name: "Indigenous Peoples / Tribal Rights Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "特定indigenous / tribal people或community在適用憲法、條約、ILO Convention No.169、UNDRIP、歷史treaty、domestic indigenous law或其他法源下，如何取得collective identity、self-government、participation、culture、land / territory / resource、traditional use與development decision中的法律位置；國家或企業推動採礦、林業、保育、基礎建設、移轉／搬遷或其他措施時，何種consultation、accommodation、FPIC或benefit-sharing義務被觸發，誰能代表community、效果與remedy又是什麼？"

  historical_reason:
    "一般個人人權與一般土地所有權不足以描述原住民族法律位置：許多爭議不是一個人對一塊登記土地，而是一個people對傳統territory、文化、制度、資源與國家歷史關係的集體主張。殖民、強制移轉、同化與資源開發又使『國家依法批准project』不能自然等於 indigenous rights 已被處理。現代法因此發展self-identification、collective institutions、traditional lands、consultation、FPIC in specified contexts、cultural rights與collective remedies。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識people / community與真正適用的legal source，不先假定所有自稱community都取得同一法位；再固定traditional institution、land / territory / resource connection及被提出的State/project action，判斷consultation / consent標準、representation、impact、benefit與remedy。不得把public hearing簽到表當FPIC，也不得把FPIC縮成全球一律單方veto。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "state sovereignty vs indigenous self-determination"
    - "registered title vs traditional territory"
    - "resource development vs collective land / culture"
    - "public consultation vs indigenous-specific consultation"
    - "consultation vs consent / FPIC"
    - "community representation vs internal diversity"
    - "collective rights vs individual member rights"
    - "conservation vs customary use / displacement"
    - "cultural preservation vs living cultural change"
    - "benefit-sharing vs commodification"
    - "national development vs historical redress"

  shortest_maxim:
    "原住民族權利第一眼不是『有沒有開公聽會』；先問『哪一個people、哪個法源、哪片territory／resource、誰代表community、國家要做什麼、何種consultation或consent門檻被觸發、結果如何留下可驗證紀錄』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-50.1 第一眼｜PEOPLE / COMMUNITY → STATUS / SOURCE → INSTITUTION → LAND / TERRITORY / RESOURCE → STATE / PROJECT ACTION → CONSULTATION → CONSENT / FPIC WHERE APPLICABLE → CULTURE / IDENTITY → IMPACT / BENEFIT → REMEDY

```text
PEOPLE / COMMUNITY
＝which indigenous / tribal people? self-identification? objective/legal criteria where applicable?

STATUS / SOURCE
＝constitution? treaty? domestic law? ILO C169? UNDRIP? historic treaty?

INSTITUTION
＝traditional authority? elected council? representative body? internal process?

LAND / TERRITORY / RESOURCE
＝registered land? traditional occupation/use? hunting/fishing? sacred site? subsurface resource?

STATE / PROJECT ACTION
＝licence? concession? conservation? relocation? legislation? infrastructure?

CONSULTATION
＝when? through whom? information? language? time? good faith?

CONSENT / FPIC WHERE APPLICABLE
＝is consent legally required under exact source / context?

CULTURE / IDENTITY
＝language? ceremony? heritage? education? institutions?

IMPACT / BENEFIT
＝environmental / social / cultural / livelihood / revenue?

REMEDY
＝stay? annulment? consultation redo? restitution? compensation? benefit-sharing?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-50.18 Evidence architecture

- recognition / status materials；
- historical treaty / statute；
- community constitution / customary institution records；
- genealogy / membership where legally relevant；
- maps；
- traditional-use evidence；
- oral history where admissible；
- land / resource records；
- archaeological / cultural evidence；
- consultation notice；
- language / translation；
- meeting record；
- representative mandate；
- technical studies；
- EIA / social impact；
- alternatives；
- community resolutions；
- consent / objection record；
- benefit-sharing agreement；
- licence / concession；
- relocation plan；
- remedy / court / treaty-body materials。

核心：

> **原住民族權利案件不能用『政府有開會』或『community有人簽名』閉合；關鍵是法源、代表性、territorial connection與決策過程是否真正取得資格。**


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-11／INT-12 的 RE_ROUTE_STRUCTURAL 決定，家事法作為 FAMILY_JUSTICE_ROUTER 的主住所置於 E；具體法院／爭端程序仍 cross-route B，不在本卡複製第二份程序法 authoritative body。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-037-FAMILY_LAW_FAMILY_JUSTICE"
  chinese_name: "家事法"
  english_name: "Family Law / Family Justice"

  domain_core_question:
    "家庭關係因分居、離婚、親職衝突、兒童照護、扶養、家庭暴力、收養或跨境移動進入法律制度時，法院如何在成人權利、兒童最佳利益、安全、持續照護與程序公平之間形成可執行安排？"

  historical_reason:
    "家庭關係不是一次性契約。成人與兒童之間具有長期依賴、情感、照護、共同生活與權力不對等。普通財產／契約法不足以處理兒童無法自己議價、家庭暴力可能扭曲表面同意、父母分離後仍須共同承擔親職，以及跨境帶離可能迅速改變兒童生活中心等問題。因此家事法以兒童最佳利益、親職責任、安全、扶養、程序參與與專門家事司法方式介入。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "父母權利 vs 兒童最佳利益"
    - "家庭自治 vs 國家保護"
    - "親職持續性 vs 安全"
    - "兒童聲音 vs 兒童不承擔成人決策責任"
    - "穩定生活環境 vs 父母遷徙自由"
    - "快速保護 vs 程序公平"
    - "家庭隱私 vs 家暴／虐待介入"
    - "共同親職 vs 高衝突／暴力風險"
    - "跨境移動 vs habitual residence / return mechanisms"
    - "扶養義務 vs 實際給付能力"

  shortest_maxim:
    "家事法第一眼不是『誰比較愛孩子』；先問『孩子與家庭目前的法律關係、安全、主要照護、生活連續性、親職能力、孩子聲音與可執行安排是什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 43.1 第一眼｜RELATIONSHIP → CHILD / DEPENDENT → SAFETY → CARE HISTORY → RESPONSIBILITY → VOICE → PRACTICAL PLAN → REVIEW

```text
RELATIONSHIP
＝父母？實際照顧者？監護人？收養？寄養？其他家庭關係？

CHILD / DEPENDENT
＝有沒有未成年人或需要特別保護的家庭成員？

SAFETY
＝家暴？虐待？疏忽？控制？威脅？成癮？重大照護風險？

CARE HISTORY
＝誰長期照顧？學校、醫療、作息、生活中心在哪？

RESPONSIBILITY
＝誰具有親職／監護／扶養義務？

VOICE
＝兒童有沒有適齡且不受壓迫地表達意見？

PRACTICAL PLAN
＝居住、會面、交通、學校、醫療、假期、資訊分享怎麼執行？

REVIEW
＝安排是否需要定期檢視或因情勢變更重開？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 43.16 證據特徵

- 出生／親子文件；
- 居住紀錄；
- 學校；
- 醫療；
- childcare；
- message；
- handover logs；
- travel records；
- police reports；
- protection orders；
- social worker reports；
- child interviews；
- financial records；
- support payments；
- custody orders；
- passports；
- habitual residence evidence；
- expert family assessment。

核心：

> **家事案件的證據目標不是證明誰比較像「好人」，而是證明安全、照護、關係與可執行安排。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-038-MARRIAGE_KINSHIP_LAW"
  chinese_name: "婚姻親屬法"
  english_name: "Marriage & Kinship Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "婚姻、伴侶、親子與親屬身分何時被法律承認；這些身分如何產生扶養、代理、財產、繼承與照護效果，又如何在離婚、親子認定、收養、生殖科技與跨境家庭中被變更或終止？"

  historical_reason:
    "親密關係與血緣本來是社會事實，但法律需要決定哪些關係取得婚姻、親子、親屬與配偶身分，因為這些身分會影響財產、扶養、醫療代理、國籍、稅、繼承與兒童照護。現代法同時面臨婚姻自主、性別平等、多元家庭、DNA、生殖科技與跨境承認等挑戰，因此不能再只靠傳統角色或生物血緣作唯一答案。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "婚姻自主 vs 法定成立要件"
    - "家庭傳統 vs 性別平等"
    - "身分穩定 vs 真實血緣／意圖"
    - "婚姻契約自由 vs 弱勢配偶保護"
    - "夫妻財產自治 vs 家庭共同貢獻"
    - "生物親子 vs 法律親子"
    - "生殖科技意圖 vs 出生／遺傳事實"
    - "離婚自由 vs 經濟後果與扶養"
    - "國內家庭身分 vs 跨境承認"
    - "宗教／文化婚姻 vs 國家民事婚姻制度"

  shortest_maxim:
    "婚姻親屬法第一眼不是『他們是不是一家人』；先問『哪一種法律身分、如何成立、是否有效、帶來哪些扶養／財產／親子效果、如何變更或終止』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 44.1 第一眼｜STATUS → FORMATION → VALIDITY → RIGHTS / DUTIES → PROPERTY → PARENTAGE → DISSOLUTION → RECOGNITION

```text
STATUS
＝婚姻？registered partnership？cohabitation？parent-child？adoption？kinship？

FORMATION
＝登記？儀式？年齡？能力？自由同意？

VALIDITY
＝重婚？禁止親等？脅迫？虛偽？形式欠缺？

RIGHTS / DUTIES
＝扶養？共同生活？醫療代理？姓氏？身份效果？

PROPERTY
＝夫妻財產制度？

PARENTAGE
＝生物？推定？認領？判決？收養？生殖科技？

DISSOLUTION
＝離婚？撤銷？死亡？

RECOGNITION
＝外國婚姻／離婚／親子判決是否承認？
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 FAMILY_STATUS_DEPENDENCY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-175-MATRIMONIAL_PROPERTY_DIVORCE_PROPERTY_DIVISION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "夫妻財產／離婚財產清算法"
  english_name: "Matrimonial Property / Divorce Property Division Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "婚姻或受法律承認的伴侶關係中，哪些資產、負債、增值、收入、事業權益、退休利益與第三人權利進入夫妻財產制度；在離婚、分居、婚姻無效或死亡前後，應依哪個財產制度、協議、準據法與清算規則分類、估價、分配或補償？"

  historical_reason:
    "家庭中的經濟貢獻不只表現在名義持有與現金收入。照護、家務、共同消費、共同負債、事業累積與職涯犧牲可能跨越多年，而財產登記往往只呈現最終名義。各法域因此發展 community property、separation、participation、equitable distribution、婚姻財產協議與各式清算制度。夫妻財產法的功能不是宣告誰比較值得，而是把身分、資產來源、時間、債務、第三人與法定分配規則接回同一條可審計鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先固定婚姻／伴侶法律身分、財產制度、重要日期與準據法，再逐項建立資產負債清冊；對每一項財產分開問來源、取得時間、名義、資金流、增值、混同、協議、第三人權利與估價日期，最後才進分配／補償。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "formal title vs economic contribution"
    - "individual property autonomy vs marital solidarity"
    - "predictability vs equitable adjustment"
    - "homemaking / caregiving contribution vs market income"
    - "premarital property vs marital appreciation"
    - "contractual autonomy vs vulnerable-spouse protection"
    - "asset concealment vs privacy"
    - "business continuity vs fair division"
    - "pension security vs present-value division"
    - "domestic regime vs cross-border property"
    - "spouse-spouse allocation vs third-party creditor rights"

  shortest_maxim:
    "夫妻財產第一眼不是『房子登記誰名下』；先問『適用哪套財產制度、哪個時間點、資產從哪裡來、是否混同或增值、負債與第三人權利是什麼、如何估價與清算』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-189.1 第一眼｜STATUS → REGIME → DATE → ASSET / DEBT MAP → SOURCE → CHARACTER → AGREEMENT → VALUE → THIRD PARTY → DIVISION

```text
STATUS
＝marriage? registered partnership? recognized status?

REGIME
＝community? separation? participation? equitable distribution? other?

DATE
＝marriage? separation? filing? valuation? dissolution?

ASSET / DEBT MAP
＝real estate? cash? securities? company shares? pension? debt?

SOURCE
＝premarital? earnings? gift? inheritance? loan? business?

CHARACTER
＝separate? marital? mixed? reimbursable contribution?

AGREEMENT
＝prenup? postnup? property agreement?

VALUE
＝which date / method / discount / tax?

THIRD PARTY
＝bank? company? trust? creditor? co-owner?

DIVISION
＝allocation? equalization? sale? transfer? compensation?
```

# DLM-176｜親職責任／監護居住會面與遷居法


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-176-PARENTAL_RESPONSIBILITY_CUSTODY_CONTACT_RELOCATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "親職責任／監護居住會面與遷居法"
  english_name: "Parental Responsibility / Custody, Contact & Relocation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "父母或其他照顧者分居、離婚或跨境生活後，誰依法具有 parental responsibility／guardianship，兒童主要居住、重大決策、日常照護、會面、資訊取得與遷居如何配置；法院又如何在兒童最佳利益、安全、兒童聲音、照護連續性與父母權利之間形成可執行、可調整的安排？"

  historical_reason:
    "父母分離不會消滅兒童持續被照護、受教育、接受醫療與維持重要關係的需要。傳統以『監護權歸誰』作單一勝負容易把兒童變成成人權利的標的；現代制度因此逐步把 parental responsibility、residence、contact、decision-making、relocation 與 child protection 分開。跨境移動更使 habitual residence、jurisdiction、return proceedings 與 recognition 成為獨立問題。"

  first_move:
    "先確認誰已依法具有 parental responsibility／guardianship，再固定兒童 habitual residence、現有命令、主要照護歷史與安全狀態；之後分開處理居住、重大決策、日常照護、contact、relocation、兒童聲音與跨境承認，不能只問『孩子判給誰』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "parental autonomy vs child best interests"
    - "continuity of care vs future adaptability"
    - "meaningful relationship with both parents vs safety"
    - "child voice vs adult burden-shifting"
    - "mobility / relocation freedom vs relationship continuity"
    - "shared responsibility vs high conflict"
    - "speed vs procedural fairness"
    - "privacy vs child-protection information"
    - "domestic orders vs cross-border recognition"
    - "stability vs modification when circumstances change"

  shortest_maxim:
    "親職第一眼不是『監護權判給誰』；先問『誰有法律責任、孩子現在在哪裡生活、誰一直照顧、風險在哪、孩子怎麼說、每一項決策與會面怎麼真正執行』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-190.1 第一眼｜LEGAL PARENT / CARER → RESPONSIBILITY → HABITUAL RESIDENCE → SAFETY → CARE HISTORY → CHILD VOICE → RESIDENCE → DECISION → CONTACT → RELOCATION → REVIEW

```text
LEGAL PARENT / CARER
＝parent? guardian? adoptive parent? other legal carer?

RESPONSIBILITY
＝who has parental responsibility / guardianship?

HABITUAL RESIDENCE
＝where is child's actual life centre for cross-border purposes?

SAFETY
＝violence? abuse? neglect? coercive control? abduction risk?

CARE HISTORY
＝who actually provided daily care?

CHILD VOICE
＝age / maturity / freedom from pressure?

RESIDENCE
＝where will child live?

DECISION
＝education? health? religion? passport? travel?

CONTACT
＝when / where / supervised?

RELOCATION
＝domestic or international move?

REVIEW
＝modification threshold / changed circumstances?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-190.15 Evidence architecture

- birth / parentage documents；
- parental-responsibility order；
- residence/contact orders；
- school records；
- medical records；
- childcare records；
- calendars / care logs；
- communications；
- travel / passport records；
- handover records；
- police / protection-order records；
- social-worker reports；
- expert assessments；
- child-hearing record；
- housing / work schedule；
- relocation plan；
- proposed transport / contact plan；
- habitual-residence evidence；
- foreign judgments / certificates。

核心：

> **親職案件不是證明哪一方更討喜，而是證明孩子的安全、照護連續性、法律責任與可執行生活安排。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 FAMILY_STATUS_DEPENDENCY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-177-FAMILY_MAINTENANCE_CHILD_SPOUSAL_SUPPORT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "家庭扶養／子女與配偶扶養法"
  english_name: "Family Maintenance / Child & Spousal Support Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "何種親子、婚姻、伴侶或其他法定家庭關係產生扶養／maintenance obligation；義務人、受領人、需求、所得、照護貢獻、計算、期間、變更、欠款與執行如何認定；跨境時又由哪個法院、準據法、中央機關與承認執行制度處理？"

  historical_reason:
    "家庭關係會產生長期經濟依賴，尤其兒童本身不能透過契約與市場收入完整承擔生活成本。配偶或伴侶分離後，也可能因照護分工、職涯中斷、疾病或制度設計產生過渡或較長期支持需求。扶養因此不是成人情感賞罰，而是依法定關係、需求、能力與政策形成的持續義務。跨境家庭則使義務認定與實際追收分離，促成中央機關與國際回收制度。"

  first_move:
    "先確認扶養義務的法律來源與關係，再分 child support、spousal / partner maintenance、other family maintenance；對每一條路徑固定義務人、受領人、income / resources、needs、care contribution、計算規則、order date、arrears、modification與enforcement，最後再處理跨境管轄與追收。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "child needs vs parental resources"
    - "predictable formula vs individual circumstances"
    - "care contribution vs cash contribution"
    - "economic self-sufficiency vs relational dependency"
    - "finality vs changing income"
    - "effective collection vs debtor minimum living needs"
    - "private settlement vs child / public interest"
    - "domestic family law vs cross-border enforcement"
    - "privacy of finances vs disclosure necessity"
    - "support duty vs contact conflict"

  shortest_maxim:
    "扶養第一眼不是『誰比較有錢就付』；先問『哪個法律關係產生義務、誰對誰、按什麼需求與資力、算多久、何時能改、欠款怎麼追、跨境如何讓命令真正流動』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-191.1 第一眼｜RELATION → DUTY → BENEFICIARY → INCOME / RESOURCES → NEEDS → CARE → FORMULA → ORDER → ARREARS → MODIFICATION → ENFORCEMENT

```text
RELATION
＝parent-child? spouse? former spouse? partner? other kinship?

DUTY
＝statutory? court-ordered? agreement?

BENEFICIARY
＝child? spouse? former spouse? dependent relative?

INCOME / RESOURCES
＝salary? business? benefits? capital? imputed income?

NEEDS
＝housing? food? education? health? special needs?

CARE
＝who bears day-to-day care / in-kind support?

FORMULA
＝guideline? judicial discretion? combination?

ORDER
＝amount? frequency? duration? indexation?

ARREARS
＝when accrued? interest? limitation? defences?

MODIFICATION
＝income / care / needs changed?

ENFORCEMENT
＝wage attachment? asset seizure? licence / administrative tools? cross-border?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-191.16 Evidence architecture

- parentage / marriage / divorce records；
- support order / agreement；
- income tax returns；
- payslips；
- bank statements；
- company financial records；
- benefits；
- childcare expenses；
- school costs；
- medical / special-needs expenses；
- housing costs；
- parenting schedule；
- payment history；
- arrears ledger；
- employment history；
- evidence of job loss / disability；
- foreign order；
- service / notice；
- Central Authority correspondence；
- enforcement record。

核心：

> **家庭扶養案要證明的是義務來源、需求、資力、照護與支付狀態；不是證明哪一方在感情上比較有理。**

```yaml
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_III_DLM_184_204"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "HCCH_PARENTAGE_SURROGACY_PROJECT_2026_NOT_ADVANCED_TO_SPECIAL_COMMISSION__NO_HCCH_PARENTAGE_CONVENTION_OPERATIVE__DOMESTIC_AND_CROSS_BORDER_RECOGNITION_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

HCCH CGAP in March 2026 decided not to advance the Parentage / Surrogacy project to a Special Commission at this stage and asked for monitoring toward 2028. There is therefore no operative HCCH parentage convention to substitute for domestic and cross-border recognition law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **HCCH — Parentage / Surrogacy Project**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/projects/legislative-projects/parentage-surrogacy/surrogacy-2011-2015/
  - Verified point: CGAP 2026 decided not to advance to a Special Commission at this stage and asked the Permanent Bureau to monitor developments ahead of 2028.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 FAMILY_STATUS_DEPENDENCY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-186-LEGAL_PARENTAGE_ASSISTED_REPRODUCTION_SURROGACY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "親子身分／人工生殖與代孕法"
  english_name: "Legal Parentage / Assisted Reproduction & Surrogacy Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "兒童的 legal parentage 如何在自然生殖、婚姻／伴侶推定、承認、DNA爭議、donor conception、IVF、胚胎／配子捐贈與surrogacy中成立、否認、轉換或被法院／行政機關確認；genetic、gestational、intentional與legal parenthood互相衝突時如何排序；跨境時外國判決／出生文件／parentage status 如何承認，並如何與兒童身分、國籍、parental responsibility與maintenance連動？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統親子身分多以生育、婚姻推定與血緣交疊，現代 DNA 與 assisted reproductive technology 使 genetic、gestational、intentional與social parenthood可以分離。代孕又可能讓生育者、遺傳父母、預定父母、仲介與多個國家的法律同時進場。法律因此不能再把出生或 DNA 當成唯一答案，而必須建立 parentage status、consent、child identity與跨境 continuity 的專門資格鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先固定兒童出生地、habitual residence、出生方式與每位相關成人角色；分 genetic / gestational / intended / presumed / registered / adjudicated parent，再查 ART consent、donor rules、surrogacy legality／effect、parentage establishment procedure與foreign recognition。parentage確定後，才把 parental responsibility、support、nationality等效果送往其他DLM。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "biological truth vs legal status stability"
    - "genetic parentage vs gestational parentage"
    - "intent to parent vs birth-based rules"
    - "adult reproductive autonomy vs child rights"
    - "donor privacy vs child's identity interests"
    - "surrogacy autonomy vs exploitation / commodification concerns"
    - "domestic prohibition vs cross-border status continuity"
    - "public policy vs avoiding status limbo"
    - "birth registration efficiency vs contestability"
    - "medical consent vs legal-parentage consent"
    - "parentage status vs parental responsibility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "親子身分第一眼不是『DNA是誰的』；先問『誰生、誰提供遺傳材料、誰依法同意成為父母、哪個制度在何時建立parentage、哪份文件只是證明、跨境後這個身分能不能繼續被承認』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-4.1 第一眼｜CHILD / BIRTH → METHOD → ACTORS → DOMESTIC PARENTAGE RULE → CONSENT → ESTABLISHMENT / CONTEST → DOCUMENT → CROSS-BORDER RECOGNITION → CHILD EFFECTS

```text
CHILD / BIRTH
＝where / when born? status documents?

METHOD
＝sexual conception? donor insemination? IVF? embryo donation? surrogacy?

ACTORS
＝gestational person? genetic contributors? spouse? intended parents? donor?

DOMESTIC PARENTAGE RULE
＝birth rule? presumption? acknowledgment? intent? court order?

CONSENT
＝medical consent? parentage consent? withdrawn? valid?

ESTABLISHMENT / CONTEST
＝automatic? declaration? acknowledgment? judgment? adoption?

DOCUMENT
＝birth certificate? court judgment? authentic instrument?

CROSS-BORDER RECOGNITION
＝which PIL / human-rights / domestic rule?

CHILD EFFECTS
＝name? nationality? inheritance? responsibility? maintenance?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-4.17 Evidence architecture

- birth record；
- marriage / partnership record；
- acknowledgment；
- DNA / genetic testing；
- ART treatment records；
- consent forms；
- gamete / embryo donor records；
- clinic records；
- surrogacy agreement；
- payment / expense records；
- parentage order；
- pre-birth / post-birth judgment；
- adoption order if used；
- foreign judgment；
- authentic instrument；
- passport / nationality record；
- child identity records；
- court / registry correspondence；
- applicable-law evidence。

核心：

> **親子身分案件不是把生物事實、醫療同意、成人意圖與法律身分揉成一個『誰是真正父母』問題；它們是不同材料，必須依法取得不同資格。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_VAW_DOMESTIC_VIOLENCE_DIRECTIVE_IN_FORCE__TRANSPOSITION_DUE_2027_06_14__DOMESTIC_PROTECTION_ORDER_COERCIVE_CONTROL_AND_CRIMINAL_SCOPE_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Directive (EU) 2024/1385 is in force, but Member States have until 14 June 2027 to transpose it. Domestic definitions, protection orders, coercive-control offences, procedure and cross-border recognition must still be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Ending gender-based violence**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/strategy-and-policy/policies/justice-and-fundamental-rights/gender-equality/gender-based-violence/ending-gender-based-violence_en
  - Verified point: Directive (EU) 2024/1385 must be transposed by Member States by 14 June 2027.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 FAMILY_STATUS_DEPENDENCY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-188-DOMESTIC_INTIMATE_PARTNER_VIOLENCE_PROTECTION_ORDERS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "家庭暴力／親密關係暴力與保護令法"
  english_name: "Domestic / Intimate-Partner Violence & Protection Orders Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "家庭、親密關係、分居／前伴侶或其他法定關係中出現physical violence、sexual violence、threats、stalking、harassment、coercive control、economic abuse或其他法定暴力時，哪些行為與關係落入保護法範圍；如何評估即時風險、取得emergency barring / restraining / protection order、配置住宅／接觸／距離／子女／武器等條款，並處理送達、複審、違反、刑民家事程序與跨境承認？"

  historical_reason:
    "親密關係中的暴力具有重複、控制、依賴、共同住所與離開成本等結構；單看單次傷勢容易漏掉威脅、跟蹤、經濟控制與升高風險。傳統刑事程序又常必須在犯罪證明完成後才處罰，無法單獨回答『今天晚上如何先讓人安全』。因此各法域發展緊急排除、限制接觸、保護令、警察風險措施與跨境承認制度，並與刑事、親職、住宅、移民與被害人支持相互咬合。"

  first_move:
    "先固定relationship與conduct pattern，再分立即危險、持續控制、已發生傷害與未來風險；同時保存原始證據與安全資訊。之後辨識emergency / interim / final order、申請主體、證明門檻、可用條款、service與review。不得要求先完成刑事定罪才承認保護程序存在，也不得因保護令核發就反推刑事有罪已證明。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "immediate safety vs adversarial hearing"
    - "ex parte urgency vs respondent due process"
    - "victim autonomy vs protective intervention"
    - "pattern-based coercive control vs incident-based evidence"
    - "shared home rights vs emergency exclusion"
    - "child contact vs safety"
    - "confidentiality vs enforcement information"
    - "criminal proof vs civil/protective standard"
    - "false / unsupported allegation risk vs under-protection risk"
    - "cross-border mobility vs continuity of protection"
    - "police power vs judicial review"
    - "victim safety vs perpetrator property / liberty restrictions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "家暴保護第一眼不是『誰比較可怕』；先問『什麼關係、哪些具體行為、風險如何升高、現在需要哪一種安全命令、證據門檻是什麼、命令如何送達複審與執行』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-14.1 第一眼｜RELATION → CONDUCT → PATTERN → IMMEDIATE RISK → VULNERABILITY → EVIDENCE → EMERGENCY ACTION → ORDER → TERMS → SERVICE / HEARING → BREACH → CROSS-BORDER

```text
RELATION
＝spouse? former spouse? partner? former partner? cohabitant? family?

CONDUCT
＝assault? sexual violence? threat? stalking? harassment? coercive control?

PATTERN
＝isolated incident? escalation? repeated surveillance? economic control?

IMMEDIATE RISK
＝weapon? strangulation? death threat? separation escalation? access?

VULNERABILITY
＝child? pregnancy? disability? immigration dependency? isolation?

EVIDENCE
＝911/police? medical? messages? video? witness? risk assessment?

EMERGENCY ACTION
＝police removal? emergency barring? shelter? confidential address?

ORDER
＝restraining? protection? no-contact? stay-away? residence exclusion?

TERMS
＝distance? communication? children? property? firearms? workplace?

SERVICE / HEARING
＝ex parte? notice? return hearing? representation?

BREACH
＝new offence? administrative/criminal sanction? modification?

CROSS-BORDER
＝recognition mechanism?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-14.13 Evidence architecture

- police report / bodycam；
- emergency call；
- medical record；
- injury photographs；
- threatening messages；
- stalking log；
- GPS / device evidence；
- witness；
- prior incidents；
- prior orders；
- firearm / weapon evidence；
- coercive-control financial records；
- shelter record；
- child disclosures；
- risk assessment；
- order application；
- affidavit；
- service proof；
- hearing record；
- breach evidence；
- cross-border certificate。

核心：

> **家暴案件的證據既要保存單次事件，也要保存時間上的pattern；因為控制關係往往藏在事件之間。**


The 1993 HCCH Intercountry Adoption Convention remains the cross-border cooperation framework for Contracting Parties. Party status, Central Authorities, domestic implementing legislation and case-specific safeguards must be checked at runtime.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **HCCH — 1993 Intercountry Adoption Convention section**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/specialised-sections/intercountry-adoption
  - Verified point: The Convention operates through Contracting Parties, Central Authorities and domestic implementation; status/authority data are maintained by HCCH.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 FAMILY_STATUS_DEPENDENCY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-190-ADOPTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "收養法"
  english_name: "Adoption Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "何時可以依法永久重建兒童與收養人之法律親子關係；兒童是否adoptable、原生家庭是否已獲適當支持、哪些人的同意／諮詢必要、兒童如何參與、收養人是否eligible / suitable、如何matching；跨國收養時又如何透過Central Authorities、防止不當金錢利益與人口販運、取得Convention-compliant recognition？"

  historical_reason:
    "收養同時創造長期家庭關係並可能終止、限制或重組既有親子法律效果，因此不能只是成人『想成為父母』的私人契約。現代制度把兒童最佳利益、原生家庭保存、知情同意、兒童聲音、收養人適格性與司法／行政審查放在核心。跨國收養更因國境、貧富差距、中介與身分文件風險而形成HCCH 1993的合作、反販運與自動承認框架。"

  first_move:
    "先固定child status與是否adoptable，再問family-of-origin preservation / subsidiarity、必要consent、child views、prospective adopter eligibility/suitability、matching與competent authority；跨國案件再確認State of origin / receiving State、Central Authorities、HCCH 1993 scope、Article 23 certificate與recognition。不得從成人意願直接跳到法律親子成立。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "child best interests vs adult desire to adopt"
    - "family preservation vs permanent alternative family"
    - "consent autonomy vs coercion / poverty pressure"
    - "child participation vs burdening the child"
    - "speed / permanency vs procedural safeguards"
    - "domestic placement vs intercountry subsidiarity"
    - "privacy vs origins / identity information"
    - "adoption fees vs improper financial gain"
    - "cross-border recognition vs State control of status"
    - "permanency vs later access to origins"

  shortest_maxim:
    "收養第一眼不是『誰願意養』；先問『孩子依法可不可以被收養、原生家庭與同意程序是否被尊重、收養人是否適格、誰有權作成身分變更、跨國程序是否走完保障與承認鏈』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-24.1 第一眼｜CHILD STATUS → ADOPTABILITY → FAMILY PRESERVATION → CONSENT → CHILD VOICE → ADOPTER ELIGIBILITY → MATCHING → AUTHORITY → ORDER → EFFECT → CROSS-BORDER RECOGNITION

```text
CHILD STATUS
＝legal parents? guardian? care status? nationality / residence?

ADOPTABILITY
＝may this child legally be adopted?

FAMILY PRESERVATION
＝can child safely remain / return within family of origin?

CONSENT
＝whose consent? informed? free? timing? revocation?

CHILD VOICE
＝age / maturity / counselling / assent or consent?

ADOPTER ELIGIBILITY
＝age? relationship? marital status? capacity? suitability?

MATCHING
＝child-centred suitability, not buyer selection

AUTHORITY
＝court? administrative authority? accredited body?

ORDER
＝when does adoption become legally effective?

EFFECT
＝parentage? name? inheritance? parental responsibility? nationality?

CROSS-BORDER
＝HCCH 1993? Central Authority? Article 23 certificate? recognition?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-24.12 Evidence architecture

- birth certificate / parentage record；
- child-protection file；
- family-preservation assessment；
- adoptability determination；
- parent / guardian consent；
- counselling record；
- child interview / consent；
- prospective-parent home study；
- suitability assessment；
- matching record；
- accredited-body file；
- fee / payment record；
- court / administrative order；
- Central Authority correspondence；
- Article 17 approvals where applicable；
- Article 23 certificate；
- immigration / nationality record；
- post-adoption report / origins information。

核心：

> **收養不是成人取得孩子的交易；它是國家在高強度身分變更中，證明永久家庭安排取得兒童權利資格的程序。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-018-CONSUMER_PROTECTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "消費者法"
  english_name: "Consumer Protection Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當企業反覆設計商品、服務、契約條款、資訊與交易流程，而一般消費者只進場一次、資訊有限且談判成本高時，法律如何讓市場選擇仍具有真實意義，而不是把所有風險都塞進『你已同意』？"

  historical_reason:
    "大量消費市場透過標準化商品與契約降低交易成本，但也使企業可以一次設計條款與資訊架構，套用到數以萬計的消費者。單一消費者通常沒有能力檢驗複雜商品、重新談條款或辨識隱藏風險。消費者法因此透過資訊、產品安全、不公平條款、冷靜期、品質責任、廣告、公平交易與集體救濟等工具修正結構性資訊與議價不對等。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "契約自由 vs 強制消費者保護"
    - "低交易成本 vs 標準條款濫用"
    - "資訊揭露 vs 資訊過載"
    - "企業創新 vs 商品／服務安全"
    - "個人選擇責任 vs dark patterns／行為操控"
    - "快速電商 vs 身分、付款、退貨與跨境救濟"
    - "個別小額損害 vs 大規模集體侵害"
    - "廣告說服 vs 誤導"
    - "個人化 vs 價格／資料歧視"

  shortest_maxim:
    "消費者法第一眼不是『他有沒有按同意』；先問『企業控制了什麼資訊、條款與交易流程，消費者是否真的有可理解、可拒絕、可救濟的選擇』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 24.1 第一眼｜CONSUMER STATUS → PRODUCT / SERVICE → INFORMATION → TERM → PRACTICE → HARM → REDRESS

```text
CONSUMER STATUS
＝誰是消費者？企業用途還是私人用途？

PRODUCT / SERVICE
＝實體商品？數位內容？訂閱？金融？平台？醫療？旅行？

INFORMATION
＝價格、風險、功能、限制、續訂、資料使用是否清楚？

TERM
＝標準條款？免責？自動續約？爭端條款？

PRACTICE
＝廣告、推銷、dark pattern、評論、價格呈現？

HARM
＝財產、人身、時間、資料、選擇、交易機會？

REDRESS
＝退款、修理、更換、解約、損賠、申訴、集體救濟？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 24.12 證據特徵

- 廣告截圖；
- 商品頁；
- 價格頁；
- 結帳流程錄影；
- 訂單；
- 發票；
- 條款版本；
- 取消流程；
- 客服；
- 錄音；
- 產品標示；
- 安全警告；
- 檢驗；
- 故障；
- 召回；
- 評論；
- 平台商家身分；
- 退款；
- chargeback；
- 使用者介面 A/B test；
- 消費者投訴統計。

核心：

> **消費者法常必須保存「交易當時使用者看到的畫面」，而不是只看事後公司提供的條款。**

## 24.14 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與交易型態現查：

1. 消費者定義；
2. 商品／服務範圍；
3. 強制資訊揭露；
4. 標準契約公平控制；
5. 瑕疵／保固；
6. 產品責任；
7. 冷靜期／撤回權；
8. 電子商務；
9. 自動續約；
10. 廣告與不公平商業行為；
11. 數位內容；
12. 金融消費者；
13. 平台市場；
14. dark patterns；
15. 集體救濟；
16. 跨境消費；
17. 主管機關執法；
18. UNGCP / OECD 建議之本國實施。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EDUCATION_LAW_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__DIGITAL_STUDENT_DATA_EQUALITY_DISABILITY_AND_SAFETY_RULES_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Education law remains primarily domestic. Digital education, student data, equality/disability accommodation and campus-safety duties must be verified in the governing jurisdiction rather than inferred from a global education label.

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date and local positive law before merits closure.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:
  domain_id: "DLM-049-EDUCATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "教育法"
  english_name: "Education Law"
  domain_core_question: "國家與教育機構在提供義務教育、高等教育、技職與私人教育時，如何分配入學、學習、評量、紀律、資格與資源；學生、家長、教師與學校各自取得什麼權利與權力，而這些教育專業決定何時必須接受平等、程序、安全、兒童與身心障礙權利的法律審查？"
  historical_reason: "教育同時是人權、公共服務、資格形成制度與社會資源分配機制。學校必須有課程、評量、紀律與專業自主，國家也需要制定最低教育標準；但教育權力能決定誰能入學、畢業、取得資格與向上流動。教育法因此不是單純『學校管理法』，而是讓教育專業、教育平等、公共責任、家長選擇、學生權利與學術自由取得可審查邊界。"
  fundamental_tension:
    - "受教權 vs 有限教育資源"
    - "義務教育 vs 家長／家庭教育選擇"
    - "學校自治 vs 公法監督"
    - "教育專業 vs 學生程序權"
    - "學術自由 vs institution / public accountability"
    - "統一標準 vs 個別學習需要"
    - "入學選拔 vs 平等機會"
    - "校園紀律 vs 學生人格／表意／隱私"
    - "學校安全 vs 過度監控"
    - "私人教育自由 vs 最低教育品質／反歧視"
  shortest_maxim: "教育法第一眼不是『學校有沒有道理』；先問『哪一層教育、哪種學校、學生要取得什麼教育資格、誰依什麼法源作決定、標準如何形成、學生有沒有平等、程序與必要支持』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 55.1 第一眼｜LEVEL → PROVIDER → LEARNER → RIGHT / ENTITLEMENT → DECISION → STANDARD → PROCEDURE → SUPPORT → REMEDY

```text
LEVEL＝幼兒？義務教育？高中？技職？大學？研究所？終身教育？
PROVIDER＝公立？私立？宗教？國際？線上？home schooling？
LEARNER＝兒童？成年人？身心障礙學生？外國學生？
RIGHT / ENTITLEMENT＝免費／義務教育？入學資格？特定學位？補助？
DECISION＝招生？分班？評量？退學？懲處？學位？
STANDARD＝課程、成績、行為、專業資格依什麼規則？
PROCEDURE＝通知、陳述、申覆、閱卷、利益衝突？
SUPPORT＝語言、disability accommodation、經濟支持？
REMEDY＝校內申訴？主管機關？行政／民事／憲法法院？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 55.16 證據特徵

- education statute；school regulations；admission criteria；exam rubric；grading record；disciplinary file；notice / appeal；support plan；accommodation request；accessibility audit；bullying report；CCTV / device policy；AI vendor documentation；accreditation；degree / transcript。

核心：

> **教育爭議不是只看結果「有沒有錄取／畢業」，而是看教育資格如何透過規則、專業判斷與程序形成。**

必須依法域與教育層級現查：right to education、compulsory education、school choice、public/private obligations、admission、tuition/scholarships、discrimination、disability accommodation、student discipline、grading、student speech/privacy、bullying/safeguarding、inclusive education、teacher law、higher-education autonomy、academic freedom、accreditation、degree recognition、automated decisions、appeal/judicial review。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-016-LABOUR_EMPLOYMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "勞動法"
  english_name: "Labour / Employment Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當一個人以持續提供勞務取得主要生計，並在他人組織、指揮、時間、報酬與風險配置之下工作時，法律為什麼不能只把雙方視為議價能力完全對等的契約當事人？"

  historical_reason:
    "勞動契約表面上是自願交換，但工作者往往以勞務維持生計，且雇主控制工作組織、資訊、考核、時間、解僱與生產風險。若只靠一般契約自由，最低工資、工時、安全、休假、歧視、解僱與集體談判等問題容易被轉化成『你已經同意』。勞動法因此透過最低標準、身份認定、集體權利與解僱控制，重新配置契約自由與結構性不對等。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "契約自由 vs 最低勞動標準"
    - "企業管理權 vs 勞工人格與基本權"
    - "雇主組織效率 vs 工時、休息與安全"
    - "解僱自由 vs 生計穩定與程序保障"
    - "個別議價 vs 集體談判"
    - "形式承攬／平台合作 vs 實質僱傭關係"
    - "企業彈性 vs 非典型勞動者保護"
    - "工作場所紀律 vs 平等、反歧視與隱私"

  shortest_maxim:
    "勞動法第一眼不是『契約叫什麼』；先問『誰控制工作、誰承擔經營風險、這個人是否在組織上與經濟上依賴他人提供生計』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 22.1 第一眼｜RELATIONSHIP → CONTROL → DEPENDENCY → TERM → EVENT

勞動案件第一步不是讀契約標題。

先拆：

```text
RELATIONSHIP
＝僱傭？承攬？委任？派遣？平台工作？實習？合作？

CONTROL / DIRECTION
＝誰決定時間、地點、方法、考核、價格、客戶、停權？

ECONOMIC DEPENDENCY
＝工作者能否自行定價、找客戶、承擔盈虧、自由替代履行？

TERM
＝工資、工時、休息、假別、獎金、職務、地點、安全？

EVENT
＝解僱？調職？減薪？工傷？歧視？欠薪？集體爭議？
```

ILO Employment Relationship Recommendation No. 198 明確指出，勞動法處理的問題之一正是僱傭關係中可能存在的不平等議價位置，並要求各國提供辨識僱傭關係的政策與指引。

所以：

```text
CONTRACT LABEL
≠ EMPLOYMENT STATUS
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ILO_C155_C187_FUNDAMENTAL_STATUS_CURRENT__RATIFICATIONS_CONTINUE_2026__DOMESTIC_OSH_SECTOR_AND_TECH_RULES_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

ILO Conventions 155 and 187 are treated as fundamental occupational-safety instruments, while ratifications continued in 2026. Concrete exposure limits, psychosocial-risk duties, inspection powers and sector standards remain domestic/sector-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ILO NORMLEX — Latest ratifications**
  - URL: https://normlex.ilo.org/dyn/nrmlx_en/f?p=NORMLEXPUB:11400:0::NO:::
  - Verified point: 2026 ratifications include further C155/C187 occupational-safety ratifications, confirming live country status.
- **ILO — NORMLEX**
  - URL: https://normlex.ilo.org/
  - Verified point: Official ILO source for instrument status, ratifications, supervisory comments and national labour-law links.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-092-OCCUPATIONAL_SAFETY_HEALTH_LAW"
  chinese_name: "職業安全衛生法"
  english_name: "Occupational Safety and Health Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "在workplace / work activity中，誰控制hazard、誰 exposed、誰有能力消除／降低風險；employer、principal contractor、subcontractor、designer、manufacturer、supplier、worker、occupational-health service與inspector的 preventive duties如何分配；事故／職業病發生後如何從 immediate event 回溯到 risk assessment、system controls、training、maintenance、work organization與regulatory enforcement？"

  historical_reason:
    "勞動契約通常把生產工具、工作節奏與工作環境的控制集中在雇主／組織一方，而危害後果落在worker身體。單靠事後tort無法處理低機率高傷害、慢性暴露與系統性風險，因此OSH law把責任前移，以national policy、inspection、risk assessment、hierarchy of controls、worker participation、training、health surveillance、incident learning與continuous improvement建立preventive culture。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "production pressure vs safety"
    - "employer control vs worker autonomy"
    - "individual error vs system responsibility"
    - "formal compliance vs actual risk control"
    - "PPE vs elimination / engineering controls"
    - "contracting flexibility vs multi-employer coordination"
    - "worker participation vs managerial prerogative"
    - "safety reporting vs retaliation"
    - "privacy vs health surveillance"
    - "productivity metrics vs fatigue / psychosocial risk"
    - "innovation / automation vs new hazards"
    - "temporary work vs equal protection"
    - "criminal enforcement vs learning culture"

  shortest_maxim:
    "職安法第一眼不是『誰操作錯了』；先問『hazard是什麼、誰控制source / workplace / task、哪些人暴露、原本有哪些higher-order controls可用、risk assessment與actual work是否一致、事故前有哪些warning / near miss被看見或壓掉』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 98.1 第一眼｜WORK ACTIVITY → HAZARD → EXPOSED PERSON → CONTROL / DUTY HOLDER → RISK ASSESSMENT → CONTROL HIERARCHY → TRAINING / COMPETENCE → MONITORING → INCIDENT / DISEASE → INVESTIGATION → REMEDY

```text
WORK ACTIVITY
＝construction？factory？office？platform / delivery？mine？farm？

HAZARD
＝machine？chemical？biological？noise？heat？fall？fatigue？violence？

EXPOSED PERSON
＝employee？contractor？temp？visitor？self-employed?

CONTROL / DUTY HOLDER
＝employer？host？principal contractor？supplier？designer？

RISK ASSESSMENT
＝task-specific？current？dynamic?

CONTROL HIERARCHY
＝eliminate？substitute？engineering？administrative？PPE?

TRAINING / COMPETENCE
＝instruction？supervision？licence？language?

MONITORING
＝inspection？exposure data？health surveillance？near miss?

INCIDENT / DISEASE
＝acute injury？occupational disease？psychosocial harm?

INVESTIGATION
＝root causes？system failures？regulatory breach?

REMEDY
＝stop work？compensation？enforcement？prosecution？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置、地方制度、最新法規或動態名單，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 98.37 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 activity、worker status、hazard、country、date 現查：

1. national OSH statute；
2. C155 / C187 ratification；
3. national policy / programme；
4. employer general duty；
5. worker duty / right；
6. principal contractor / host duty；
7. designer / manufacturer / supplier duty；
8. risk assessment；
9. hierarchy of controls；
10. machine guarding；
11. lockout / permit-to-work；
12. PPE；
13. training / competency；
14. safety committees / representatives；
15. retaliation protection；
16. stop-work / imminent danger；
17. chemicals / exposure limits；
18. biological hazards；
19. noise / vibration；
20. heat；
21. ergonomics；
22. psychosocial risks；
23. violence / harassment；
24. working time / fatigue；
25. temporary / migrant / platform workers；
26. health surveillance；
27. incident / near-miss reporting；
28. inspectors / stop orders；
29. administrative / criminal offences；
30. workers' compensation / social insurance；
31. civil liability；
32. limitation / records；
33. appeals / judicial review；
34. sector-specific standards such as construction / mining / agriculture where applicable。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_PLATFORM_WORK_DIRECTIVE_IN_FORCE__TRANSPOSITION_DUE_2026_12_02__LEGAL_PRESUMPTION_CUTOVER_2026_12_02__DOMESTIC_STATUS_TEST_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Directive (EU) 2024/2831 must be transposed by 2 December 2026. For pre-existing contractual relationships the Directive’s legal presumption applies only to the period from that date, so today’s status remains transition plus domestic employment-status law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Directive (EU) 2024/2831 on platform work**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/2831/oj
  - Verified point: Transposition is due 2 December 2026; the legal presumption for ongoing pre-existing contracts applies only from that date.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-169-EMPLOYMENT_STATUS_PLATFORM_NONSTANDARD_WORK_LAW"
  chinese_name: "僱傭身分／平台與非典型勞動法"
  english_name: "Employment Status / Platform & Non-Standard Work Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一提供勞務者在特定法域、特定制度與特定時間下究竟屬employee／worker／dependent contractor／self-employed／agency worker／platform worker或其他受保護身分；誰真正控制價格、工時、工作分派、績效、客戶與停權；形式契約、企業結構、演算法管理與實際經濟依賴如何共同決定哪些勞動權利、社會保障、集體權與資料治理規則適用？"

  historical_reason:
    "勞動法的入口不是職稱，而是關係。企業可以透過承攬、派遣、加盟、外包、零工、平台與多層供應鏈重新配置形式責任，但工作者仍可能在經濟上依賴、在組織上被整合、在價格與工作分配上受控制。數位平台又把傳統主管命令部分改寫成rating、ranking、matching、surge pricing、automated suspension與deactivation。若法律只看契約標題，就會讓取得勞動保障的資格由可能被規範的一方自行命名。"

  first_move:
    "先固定真正的工作關係與控制來源：誰取得工作、誰定價、誰排程、誰承擔盈虧、誰能找替代人、誰能拒絕任務、誰能停權；再依exact domestic test判status。不要從『平台』『自由接案』『自己有工具』或『簽了承攬』直接得出結論。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "contract form vs substantive employment relationship"
    - "managerial control vs entrepreneurial independence"
    - "flexibility vs minimum labour protection"
    - "algorithmic management vs human accountability"
    - "multi-apping vs economic dependency"
    - "personal service vs genuine substitution"
    - "platform intermediation vs employer function"
    - "agency / subcontracting vs allocation of employer duties"
    - "worker protection vs genuine self-employment"
    - "labour status vs social-security / tax status"
    - "automated deactivation vs procedural fairness"
    - "data-driven management vs privacy / transparency"

  shortest_maxim:
    "僱傭身分第一眼不是『契約叫什麼』；先問工作到底怎麼被組織、控制、計價與停止，再依法域把實際關係映射到法律身分。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-169.1 第一眼｜PERSON → CONTRACT FORM → ACTUAL CONTROL → ECONOMIC RISK → PERSONAL SERVICE → INTEGRATION → STATUS TEST → RIGHTS CONSEQUENCE

```text
PERSON
＝employee? contractor? courier? driver? agency worker? freelancer? franchisee?

CONTRACT FORM
＝employment agreement? service contract? platform terms? agency contract?

ACTUAL CONTROL
＝price? schedule? route? task allocation? customer access? rating? discipline?

ECONOMIC RISK
＝who bears loss? buys equipment? can set price? build own clientele?

PERSONAL SERVICE
＝must perform personally? genuine substitution right?

INTEGRATION
＝part of employer business or independent business on own account?

STATUS TEST
＝exact domestic statutory / case-law test?

RIGHTS CONSEQUENCE
＝wage/time? dismissal? collective rights? social insurance? tax? OSH?
```

核心：

```text
CONTRACT LABEL
!= EMPLOYMENT STATUS
```

- signed contract / platform terms；
- onboarding material；
- pricing rules；
- task-allocation logs；
- acceptance / cancellation rules；
- rating / ranking rules；
- deactivation notices；
- GPS / route data；
- working-time traces；
- invoices / payroll；
- tax filings；
- social-security registration；
- equipment ownership；
- substitution practice；
- customer ownership；
- exclusivity / multi-app evidence；
- communications with managers；
- algorithmic decision records；
- collective agreements；
- statutory presumptions。

核心：

> **僱傭身分常由整段工作關係證明，而不是由 onboarding 當天簽的那張紙證明。**

Directive (EU) 2023/970 required transposition by 7 June 2026, while employer reporting starts in phases from 7 June 2027 and 2031. The transposition deadline having passed does not prove uniform national implementation; working-time, wage and leave rules remain domestic.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-170-WAGES_WORKING_TIME_REST_LEAVE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "工資／工時／休息休假法"
  english_name: "Wages / Working Time / Rest & Leave Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一勞務期間哪些時間依法屬working time／on-call／standby／overtime；哪些給付屬wage／salary／overtime premium／allowance／bonus；最低工資、正常與最高工時、休息、週休、年休、病假、家庭與育兒相關假別如何取得；契約、集體協約、工作紀錄與法定最低標準又如何相互作用？"

  historical_reason:
    "工作交換最容易被簡化成『做多少、付多少』，但工資法處理最低所得與支付安全，工時法處理健康、安全與生活時間，休息休假又處理人不可能被企業連續占用的身體與照護現實。遠距、待命、零工、分段排班與數位打卡使『工作時間』與『可支配時間』的界線更加複雜。這些規則大量具有最低強制性，不能只靠契約同意決定。"

  first_move:
    "先重建實際時間與給付：何時開始受工作義務拘束、何時真正可自由支配、哪些款項依法計入工資，再依exact statute／collective agreement計算最低工資、正常工時、加班、休息與各種假別。不要從月薪總額或員工『自願』直接完成合法性。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "freedom of contract vs mandatory labour minima"
    - "working time vs rest / private life"
    - "salary flexibility vs wage protection"
    - "on-call availability vs actual work"
    - "overtime consent vs health limits"
    - "remote work flexibility vs hidden overtime"
    - "variable pay vs equal pay / transparency"
    - "business continuity vs leave entitlement"
    - "record-keeping burden vs proof asymmetry"
    - "individual opt-out vs collective / statutory protection"
    - "productivity incentives vs predictable income"

  shortest_maxim:
    "工資工時第一眼不是『月薪多少』；先把每一段受拘束的時間與每一筆給付拆開，再問最低標準、加班、休息與假別是否依法成立。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-170.1 第一眼｜STATUS → TIME MAP → PAY MAP → MINIMUM STANDARD → OVERTIME / REST → LEAVE → RECORD → REMEDY

```text
STATUS
＝this worker is covered by which regime?

TIME MAP
＝work? on-call? standby? travel? training? waiting? breaks?

PAY MAP
＝base wage? allowance? bonus? tips? commission? overtime premium?

MINIMUM STANDARD
＝minimum wage / pay period / lawful deductions?

OVERTIME / REST
＝normal hours? max hours? daily/weekly rest? night work?

LEAVE
＝annual? sick? maternity/paternity/parental/care?

RECORD
＝timesheet? roster? system logs? payslip?

REMEDY
＝arrears? premium? penalty? injunction? administrative sanction?
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
```

ILO Convention 158 is in force and NORMLEX currently shows 37 ratifications. It binds only ratifying Members according to its terms; dismissal grounds, redundancy procedure, notice and remedies remain dependent on domestic implementation.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ILO NORMLEX — C158 Termination of Employment Convention**
  - URL: https://normlex.ilo.org/dyn/nrmlx_en/f?p=NORMLEXPUB:12201:0::NO::P12201_INSTRUMENT_ID:312303
  - Verified point: C158 entered into force in 1985 and NORMLEX showed 37 ratifications in August 2026; Convention binds ratifying Members.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-171-DISMISSAL_REDUNDANCY_EMPLOYMENT_TERMINATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "解僱／資遣與僱傭終止法"
  english_name: "Dismissal / Redundancy & Employment Termination Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "僱傭關係究竟由誰、以什麼法律原因、在何時與何種程序下終止；行為／能力／營運需求／經濟性裁員／期滿／合意／辭職／constructive dismissal如何分類；是否需要理由、警告、改善機會、通知、諮詢、選擇標準、資遣費或主管機關程序；終止違法時救濟是復職、補償、工資、資遣費或其他效果？"

  historical_reason:
    "一般契約的終止常被想像成『不合作就解約』，但僱傭終止直接切斷所得、職業身分與社會保障連結。法律因此常把valid reason、procedural fairness、notice、severance、collective consultation與anti-discrimination嵌入終止權。企業真正的重組、績效不佳與重大違紀又確實可能需要終止，因此本領域的任務不是把所有解僱視為違法，而是把『有終止意志』轉成可審核的資格鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先分類終止事件：誰發起、理由屬conduct／capacity／operational requirement／fixed-term expiry／mutual separation／resignation哪一類；再查實體理由與程序是否各自成立。不要先從『員工有錯』或『公司虧損』跳到合法終止。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "managerial flexibility vs employment security"
    - "misconduct discipline vs proportionality / due process"
    - "performance management vs disguised dismissal"
    - "operational restructuring vs sham redundancy"
    - "individual termination vs collective redundancy procedure"
    - "notice / severance vs summary dismissal"
    - "business transfer vs continuity of employment"
    - "fixed-term flexibility vs avoidance of dismissal protection"
    - "retaliation / discrimination vs legitimate reason"
    - "reinstatement vs compensation"
    - "resignation vs constructive dismissal"
    - "termination date vs accrual of final entitlements"

  shortest_maxim:
    "解僱第一眼不是『公司要不要留你』；先固定誰終止、為什麼終止、理由是否屬法律承認類型、程序是否完成，再談終止效果與救濟。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-171.1 第一眼｜RELATIONSHIP → TERMINATION ACT → INITIATOR → REASON → PROCEDURE → NOTICE / SEVERANCE → COLLECTIVE DIMENSION → REMEDY

```text
RELATIONSHIP
＝employment status confirmed?

TERMINATION ACT
＝dismissal? non-renewal? suspension? deactivation? resignation?

INITIATOR
＝employer? worker? mutual?

REASON
＝conduct? capacity? performance? operational requirement? discriminatory/retaliatory?

PROCEDURE
＝warning? hearing? improvement plan? consultation? authority notice?

NOTICE / SEVERANCE
＝period? pay in lieu? statutory severance?

COLLECTIVE DIMENSION
＝threshold? consultation? worker representatives? authority notification?

REMEDY
＝reinstatement? compensation? back pay? declaration? penalty?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-171.14 Evidence architecture

- employment contract；
- work rules / handbook；
- warnings；
- investigation file；
- performance reviews；
- KPI source data；
- improvement plan；
- medical / accommodation records where lawful；
- restructuring documents；
- board / management decision；
- headcount data；
- selection matrix；
- consultation notices；
- worker-representative records；
- authority notification；
- dismissal letter；
- notice / severance calculation；
- replacement hiring evidence；
- protected complaint / union activity chronology；
- messages / emails；
- payroll / leave balance；
- social-security transition records。

核心：

> **解僱理由最容易被事後文字重寫，所以必須保留決策形成時真正存在的資料鏈。**

6. severance；
7. redundancy definition；

9. consultation / authority notification；

11. business-transfer protection；
12. fixed-term expiry / anti-avoidance；
13. constructive dismissal；
14. anti-retaliation；
15. discrimination / protected leave；
16. union / worker-representative protection；
17. interim relief；
18. reinstatement / compensation；
19. limitation / filing deadlines；
20. C158 ratification / domestic implementation where invoked。

Freedom of association and collective bargaining remain anchored in ILO fundamental standards, but ratification, public-service limits, strike law, bargaining units and remedies vary by jurisdiction. BUILD02 therefore keeps the national/sector gate open.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **ILO — NORMLEX**
  - URL: https://normlex.ilo.org/

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 INSTITUTIONAL_PARTICIPATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-184-COLLECTIVE_LABOUR_TRADE_UNION_COLLECTIVE_BARGAINING_INDUSTRIAL_ACTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "集體勞動／工會、集體談判與爭議行動法"
  english_name: "Collective Labour / Trade Union, Collective Bargaining & Industrial Action Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "勞工與雇主如何依法組成、加入、退出與運作組織；工會／雇主組織如何取得代表性、談判資格與組織自主；集體談判如何形成團體協約及其效力；集體勞資爭議何時可透過罷工、lockout或其他 collective action 表達，又受到哪些投票、通知、和平義務、essential-services、public-service與最低服務限制？"

  historical_reason:
    "個別勞工與大型組織談判條件時常存在結構性權力差。集體組織、集體談判與爭議行動使工作條件不只由單一契約決定，也形成勞動市場中的制度性自我治理。但集體力量本身也可能影響第三人、公共服務與企業營運，因此法律同時保護結社自主並設定代表性、程序、民主決策、和平義務與必要限制。"

  first_move:
    "先分組織法、集體談判、團體協約與 industrial action 四條路徑；固定組織／會員／雇主／談判單位與適用法，再判 representativeness、anti-union protection、bargaining process、agreement effect、strike/lockout trigger、notice/ballot、essential-service restrictions 與 remedies。不得只因『很多員工一起』就叫集體勞動法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "freedom of association vs organizational regulation"
    - "collective worker power vs managerial autonomy"
    - "union independence vs State / employer interference"
    - "majority representation vs minority-union rights"
    - "collective bargaining autonomy vs statutory minima"
    - "industrial peace vs right to collective action"
    - "strike effectiveness vs continuity of essential services"
    - "union security vs individual freedom not to associate"
    - "public-service continuity vs public employees' collective rights"
    - "collective agreement stability vs changing economic conditions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "集體勞動第一眼不是『工會支持誰』；先問『誰有組織與代表資格、談判的是哪一層、協約對誰有何效力、爭議行動從哪個法源取得資格、程序與限制是否完成』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-2.1 第一眼｜ORGANIZATION → MEMBERSHIP → REPRESENTATION → BARGAINING UNIT / LEVEL → NEGOTIATION → AGREEMENT → DISPUTE → INDUSTRIAL ACTION → LIMITS → REMEDY

```text
ORGANIZATION
＝union? federation? works council? employer organization?

MEMBERSHIP
＝who can join / leave / be represented?

REPRESENTATION
＝recognition? majority? plurality? statutory representativeness?

BARGAINING UNIT / LEVEL
＝enterprise? sector? occupation? national?

NEGOTIATION
＝duty / promotion / good faith / information / consultation?

AGREEMENT
＝who is bound? duration? extension? incorporation?

DISPUTE
＝rights dispute or interests dispute?

INDUSTRIAL ACTION
＝strike? sympathy action? political strike? lockout? work-to-rule?

LIMITS
＝ballot? notice? essential service? minimum service? public service?

REMEDY
＝reinstatement? bargaining order? injunction? damages? administrative / labour-board relief?
```

- union constitution / registration；
- membership evidence；
- representative election；
- bargaining-unit data；
- recognition decision；
- collective agreement；
- bargaining minutes；
- proposals / counterproposals；
- employer communications；
- anti-union statements；
- dismissal / discipline records；
- strike ballot；
- strike notice；
- picket instructions；
- essential-service designation；
- minimum-service plan；
- lockout notice；
- mediation / conciliation record；
- labour-board / court orders。

核心：

> **集體勞動的證據不只證明「很多人不滿」，而是證明組織、代表、程序、協約與集體行動如何取得法律資格。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-017-SOCIAL_LAW_SOCIAL_PROTECTION"
  chinese_name: "社會法／社會保障法"
  english_name: "Social Law / Social Protection Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當疾病、失能、失業、育兒、老年、照護與貧窮等生活風險超出個人即時市場收入可以承受的範圍時，國家如何透過社會保險、社會救助、公共服務與最低保障，把生存風險從單一個人重新分配到社會共同體？"

  historical_reason:
    "市場交換可以分配收入，但人在童年、疾病、失業、失能、育兒與老年時可能無法持續以勞動換取生活資源。若法律只保護市場契約而不處理這些生命週期風險，人在最需要保障時反而會離開市場保護範圍。社會法因此透過社會保險、稅收型給付、公共醫療與社會服務建立最低生活與風險分擔制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "個人責任 vs 社會連帶"
    - "普遍保障 vs 財政永續"
    - "繳費對價性 vs 社會再分配"
    - "精準資格審查 vs 尊嚴與可近性"
    - "防止濫用 vs 避免排除真正需要者"
    - "中央統一標準 vs 地方差異"
    - "現金給付 vs 公共服務"
    - "立即最低保障 vs 漸進提高保障水準"

  shortest_maxim:
    "社會法第一眼不是『這個人有沒有工作』；先問『他遇到哪一種生命風險、制度用哪一種共同分擔方式接住、取得給付需要什麼法律資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 23.1 第一眼｜RISK → SCHEME → STATUS → CONTRIBUTION / NEED → BENEFIT → REVIEW

```text
SOCIAL RISK
＝疾病？生育？職災？失業？失能？老年？死亡？照護？貧窮？

SCHEME
＝社會保險？社會救助？公共醫療？家庭給付？失業保障？年金？

STATUS
＝受僱？自營？居民？公民？被扶養？兒童？老年人？身心障礙？

CONTRIBUTION / NEED
＝需繳費年資？所得／資產審查？醫療認定？失業狀態？

BENEFIT
＝現金？醫療？年金？服務？補貼？

REVIEW
＝被拒絕後如何異議、複查、訴訟？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 23.10 證據特徵

- 投保紀錄；
- 繳費年資；

- 醫療診斷；

- 家庭資料；
- 財產；

- 身份；
- 行政處分；
- 福利計算；
- 停發原因；

核心：

> **社會法案件常不是爭論抽象同情，而是爭論一個人是否進入法定風險與資格結構。**

7. 老年年金；
8. 失能／身障給付；
9. 生育／育兒；
10. 社會救助；
11. 所得與資產審查；
12. 居住／國籍資格；
13. 非典型工作者；
14. 給付追回；
15. 行政程序與救濟；
16. ILO C102 / R202 等標準之批准與國內實施。

The EU European Health Data Space Regulation is in force, but its general application begins on 26 March 2027 and major primary/secondary-use obligations phase in from 2029 and 2031. Entry into force therefore cannot be treated as present full application.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Regulation (EU) 2025/327 European Health Data Space**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2025/327/oj
  - Verified point: Regulation is in force; general application begins 26 March 2027 with substantial phased application in 2029, 2031 and 2035.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-025-MEDICAL_HEALTH_CARE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "醫療法"
  english_name: "Medical / Health Care Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當病人因疾病、傷害或不確定健康風險把身體、資訊與重大生命決定交進專業醫療系統時，法律如何在專業判斷、病人自主、照護安全、資源限制與醫療責任之間配置決策資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "醫療具有高度專業資訊不對稱，病人通常無法自行驗證診斷、治療風險與替代方案；同時醫療本身具有不確定性，即使符合專業標準也可能出現不良結果。若法律只看結果，會把醫療風險等同過失；若法律只信任專業，又可能讓病人失去自主與救濟。因此醫療法發展出知情同意、專業注意義務、保密、病歷、患者安全、醫療機構責任與生命末期決策等制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "醫療專業判斷 vs 病人自主"
    - "治療利益 vs 身體完整與拒絕權"
    - "醫療不確定性 vs 過失責任"
    - "醫療保密 vs 公共衛生／第三人安全"
    - "個別最佳照護 vs 有限醫療資源"
    - "醫療創新 vs 患者安全"
    - "緊急救治 vs 完整同意程序"
    - "生命延長 vs 尊嚴與生命末期自主"
    - "錯誤究責 vs 系統性病安改善"

  shortest_maxim:
    "醫療法第一眼不是『病人後來變好了還是變壞了』；先問『誰決定了什麼、當時知道什麼、應該說明什麼、專業標準要求什麼、結果如何從行為連到損害』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 31.1 第一眼｜RELATIONSHIP → DECISION → INFORMATION → STANDARD → EVENT → CAUSATION → HARM

醫療案件先拆：

```text
RELATIONSHIP
＝誰是病人？誰是醫師／醫療機構？有無治療關係？

DECISION
＝診斷？檢查？用藥？手術？不治療？轉診？出院？

INFORMATION
＝當時有哪些症狀、檢驗、影像、風險與替代方案？

STANDARD
＝當時在該情境下合理專業照護要求什麼？

EVENT
＝實際發生什麼不良結果？

CAUSATION
＝若沒有該行為／遺漏，結果是否有法律上重要差異？

HARM
＝身體、死亡、額外治療、失能、精神、財產？
```

所以：

```text
BAD OUTCOME
≠ AUTOMATIC MALPRACTICE
```

15. 生命末期；
16. telemedicine；

18. 臨床研究與倫理審查。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 SOCIAL_RISK_CARE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-172-OLD_AGE_STATUTORY_PENSIONS_SURVIVORS_BENEFITS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "老年／法定年金與遺屬給付法"
  english_name: "Old-Age / Statutory Pensions & Survivors' Benefits Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個人或其遺屬在何種法定社會保障制度下，依年齡、保險／繳費／居住期間、所得與家庭關係取得老年年金、退休給付、最低年金、遺屬給付或相關社會保障；跨法域工作、資格期間合併、給付計算、停發／減額與救濟又如何處理？"

  historical_reason:
    "老年會使持續以勞動換取所得的能力下降，但人的生活需要不會因此消失。現代社會保障因此建立以繳費、保險、稅收或居民資格為基礎的老年所得安全制度；同時，死亡又可能使依賴原被保險人的家庭成員失去所得來源。年金制度跨越數十年工作史、人口老化與公共財政，資格與給付計算遠比『退休就領錢』複雜。"

  first_move:
    "先確認制度種類與法律性質：statutory social-security pension、tax-financed old-age benefit、occupational pension、private pension account不是同一制度。再固定被保險人／申請人身分、年齡、繳費／保險／居住期間、跨境期間、退休／延後退休狀態、遺屬關係與計算公式。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "income security vs fiscal sustainability"
    - "contributory entitlement vs redistribution"
    - "career history vs caregiving / interrupted work"
    - "retirement age vs life expectancy / labour participation"
    - "individual account logic vs social solidarity"
    - "acquired rights / legitimate expectations vs legislative reform"
    - "national scheme autonomy vs cross-border portability"
    - "survivor protection vs changing family structures"
    - "minimum pension adequacy vs contribution proportionality"

  shortest_maxim:
    "年金第一眼不是『幾歲退休』；先問『哪個制度、什麼資格期間、哪些期間可合併、誰負責給付、用哪個公式算、跨境與遺屬效果如何』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-179.1 第一眼｜SCHEME → PERSON → QUALIFYING EVENT → PERIODS → CONTRIBUTIONS / RESIDENCE → CALCULATION → PORTABILITY → SURVIVOR → REVIEW

```text
SCHEME
＝statutory pension? residence-based benefit? occupational? private?

PERSON
＝insured person? pensioner? spouse? child? dependent survivor?

QUALIFYING EVENT
＝old age? retirement? death?

PERIODS
＝insurance? contribution? employment? residence? credited periods?

CALCULATION
＝defined benefit? points? notional account? flat-rate? minimum pension?

PORTABILITY
＝domestic only? exportable? aggregation? bilateral / regional coordination?

SURVIVOR
＝relationship? dependency? age? duration? remarriage / cohabitation effects?

REVIEW
＝calculation challenge? missing contribution record? suspension? recovery?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-179.11 Evidence architecture

- social-security account；
- contribution history；
- employment / self-employment records；
- residence records；
- credited care / military / unemployment periods；
- foreign insurance records；
- birth / age documents；
- pension calculation worksheet；
- indexation notices；
- retirement application；
- marriage / partnership records；
- death certificate；
- dependency evidence；
- administrative decision；
- recovery / overpayment notice；
- appeal record。

核心：

> **年金案件經常不是「我工作一輩子為何領不到」的抽象公平問題，而是數十年期間、身分、法源與計算公式能否被逐段接回。**

12. overlapping benefits；

14. tax treatment；

16. limitation / appeal / judicial review；

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 SOCIAL_RISK_CARE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-173-UNEMPLOYMENT_INSURANCE_BENEFITS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "失業保險／失業給付法"
  english_name: "Unemployment Insurance / Unemployment Benefits Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個人失去或減少工作後，是否落入特定失業保險／給付制度；離職原因、受保身分、保險／工作期間、可工作與求職狀態、收入、制裁與跨境紀錄如何影響資格、期間、金額與救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "失業不是單純『沒有工作』，而是勞動所得突然中斷的社會風險。社會保險制度通常用共同繳費或公共財源在失業期間提供暫時所得支持，但又必須區分非自願失業、自願離職、拒絕合適工作、部分失業與勞動市場退出。這使 unemployment benefit 同時具有所得保障與勞動市場再連結功能。"

  first_move:
    "先把『僱傭關係如何結束』與『失業給付資格』分開。第一步確認申請人是否屬受保／涵蓋人口、何時失業、為何失業、已有多少 qualifying periods、目前是否available for work／actively seeking work，再查給付、等待期、制裁、兼職收入與跨境規則。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "income security vs work incentives"
    - "insurance contribution vs labour-market assistance"
    - "involuntary unemployment vs voluntary exit"
    - "activation duties vs dignity / realistic job matching"
    - "fraud prevention vs accessible benefits"
    - "temporary support vs long-term unemployment"
    - "gig / platform work vs binary employment categories"
    - "domestic insurance history vs cross-border mobility"
    - "sanctions vs proportionality / procedural fairness"

  shortest_maxim:
    "失業給付第一眼不是『被解僱就能領』；先問『是否受保、怎麼失業、累積多少期間、現在是否符合可工作／求職條件、哪個制度要承擔這段所得中斷』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-180.1 第一眼｜COVERAGE → LOSS OF WORK → CAUSE → QUALIFYING PERIOD → AVAILABILITY → CLAIM → BENEFIT → SANCTION → REEMPLOYMENT / REVIEW

```text
COVERAGE
＝employee? self-employed? platform worker? excluded category?

LOSS OF WORK
＝total unemployment? partial? reduced hours? temporary layoff?

CAUSE
＝dismissal? redundancy? resignation? misconduct? contract expiry?

QUALIFYING PERIOD
＝insurance / employment / contribution periods?

AVAILABILITY
＝able? available? registered? actively seeking?

BENEFIT
＝amount? replacement rate? duration? waiting period?

SANCTION
＝refused suitable work? missed appointment? false statement?

REVIEW
＝appeal? overpayment? recovery? judicial review?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-180.12 Evidence architecture

- employment contract；
- termination / resignation letter；
- payroll / contributions；
- insurance record；
- unemployment registration；
- job-search logs；
- job offers / refusals；
- medical / caregiving evidence where relevant；
- platform activity logs；
- part-time earnings；
- benefit calculation；
- sanction notice；
- agency interview record；
- overpayment determination；
- cross-border forms / insurance periods；
- appeal record。

核心：

> **失業給付案件不是只問「現在有沒有工作」，而是把工作關係、受保歷史、失業原因與現在的勞動市場狀態接到同一資格鏈。**
