## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 SOCIAL_RISK_CARE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-174-SOCIAL_ASSISTANCE_MINIMUM_INCOME_PROTECTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "社會救助／最低所得保障法"
  english_name: "Social Assistance / Minimum-Income Protection Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當個人或家庭缺乏足夠資源，而社會保險、勞動所得或其他給付不足以維持法定最低生活時，是否符合社會救助／最低所得／緊急救助制度；所得、財產、家庭單位、居住、扶養、服務使用與活動要求如何影響資格、金額、停發、追回與救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "社會保險多半建立在繳費或特定受保身分上，但最脆弱的人往往正是長期無法穩定參與保險制度者。社會救助因此用需要與最低生活保障作為另一條入口，避免『沒有繳費』變成『沒有生存保障』。但 means test、家庭責任、資產審查與反詐機制也可能形成高度行政負擔、污名與錯誤排除。"

  first_move:
    "先問這是 contribution-based benefit 還是 need-based assistance。若是社會救助，再固定申請單位、法定最低生活標準、所得／資產定義、排除項、家庭／扶養關係、居住資格、其他給付優先、活動條件與緊急例外。不要從『很窮』直接跳到『依法一定可領』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "minimum dignity vs fiscal targeting"
    - "individual entitlement vs household means testing"
    - "social solidarity vs family maintenance expectations"
    - "fraud control vs take-up / accessibility"
    - "activation / conditionality vs unconditional minimum security"
    - "asset preservation vs last-resort assistance"
    - "residence / migration control vs basic needs"
    - "automation efficiency vs explainability / due process"
    - "emergency relief vs ordinary entitlement"
    - "local discretion vs national equality"

  shortest_maxim:
    "社會救助第一眼不是『他很可憐』；先問『哪個最低保障制度、誰是申請單位、哪些資源依法計入、最低需求怎麼算、其他給付與扶養關係如何影響、拒絕時能否真正救濟』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-181.1 第一眼｜SCHEME → HOUSEHOLD / CLAIMANT → NEED STANDARD → INCOME → ASSETS → OTHER SUPPORT → CONDITIONS → BENEFIT → DECISION → REVIEW

```text
SCHEME
＝minimum income? general assistance? emergency relief? housing / utility support?

CLAIMANT UNIT
＝individual? household? family? dependent unit?

NEED STANDARD
＝poverty threshold? statutory basic amount? basket of goods/services?

INCOME
＝wages? pension? maintenance? informal income? benefit exclusions?

ASSETS
＝cash? home? vehicle? savings? trust? inaccessible property?

OTHER SUPPORT
＝maintenance? family support? insurance? other public benefit?

CONDITIONS
＝residence? work-search? school? treatment? documentation?

REVIEW
＝notice? reasons? interim relief? appeal? court?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-181.14 Evidence architecture

- application form；
- household register / family relation；
- residence / immigration status；
- income records；
- bank statements；
- tax records；
- benefit statements；
- maintenance orders / payments；
- property / vehicle / savings；
- housing cost；
- utility bills；
- medical / disability / caregiving records when relevant；
- employment-service record；
- social-work assessment；
- automated matching log；
- adverse-action notice；
- fraud allegation / investigation；
- emergency-assistance record；
- appeal / interim relief record。

核心：

> **社會救助不是把人的窮困翻成一個道德分數，而是要把最低需求、可用資源、家庭單位與行政程序逐項取得法律資格。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "LTC_LAW_PRIMARILY_DOMESTIC__EU_2022_COUNCIL_RECOMMENDATION_POLICY_BASELINE__NATIONAL_PROVIDER_FUNDING_SAFEGUARDING_AND_RESIDENT_RIGHTS_REQUIRED"

```

The EU’s 2022 Council Recommendation on affordable high-quality long-term care remains a policy baseline, and a monitoring framework was first adopted in May 2025. Licensing, provider duties, funding, safeguarding, residential rights and enforcement remain national.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Long-term care**
  - URL: https://employment-social-affairs.ec.europa.eu/policies-and-activities/social-protection-social-inclusion/social-protection/long-term-care_en
  - Verified point: The 2022 Council Recommendation remains the EU policy baseline; the first monitoring framework was adopted in May 2025 and is updated regularly.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 SOCIAL_RISK_CARE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-204-LONG_TERM_CARE_CARE_FACILITY_REGULATION_SAFEGUARDING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "長期照護／照護機構監管與安全保護法"
  english_name: "Long-Term Care / Care-Facility Regulation & Safeguarding Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "因身體、認知、精神或功能能力長期下降而需要持續協助的人，如何依法取得home care、community care、residential/institutional care或其他long-term care；誰有資格成為provider、費用由公共社會保障／保險／私人支付如何分擔、needs assessment與care plan如何形成，照護中consent/capacity、staffing、quality、medication、restraint、abuse/neglect safeguarding、inspection、complaint與facility closure如何取得資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "長期照護位在醫療、社會保障、家庭照顧、身障權利與住宅生活交界。若只當醫療案件，會漏掉日常生活支持、住宿、照護人力與social-care funding；只當福利案件，又會漏掉provider licensing、品質、虐待、約束與機構監督。入住照護機構更會使一個人的home、care provider、財產、身體與決策空間高度集中於同一組織，因此需要獨立的服務治理與safeguarding鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先判care need與setting，不先以年齡命名；再固定provider legal status、funding/eligibility、assessment、care plan、consent/capacity與family/representative role。若進institutional/residential setting，立即加開staffing、quality、medication/restraint、abuse/neglect、inspection、complaint、transfer/closure。不得把『家人同意入住』直接等同本人合法同意，也不得把『有醫師照顧』等同機構品質合格。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "autonomy vs protection"
    - "home/community care vs institutional convenience"
    - "family care vs public responsibility"
    - "affordability vs workforce quality"
    - "care continuity vs provider failure"
    - "safety vs restraint / liberty"
    - "substitute support vs person's will and preferences"
    - "clinical judgment vs social-care choice"
    - "privacy vs safeguarding investigation"
    - "staff shortages vs minimum standards"
    - "public funding vs provider market sustainability"
    - "inspection transparency vs resident confidentiality"

  shortest_maxim:
    "長照第一眼不是『老人需要照顧』；先問『誰真正需要什麼長期支持、在哪個setting、哪個provider依法提供、錢怎麼付、本人如何參與care plan，機構用什麼人力與限制照護、虐待／疏忽怎麼被發現與救濟』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-74.1 第一眼｜CARE NEED → PERSON → SETTING → PROVIDER → FUNDING / ELIGIBILITY → ASSESSMENT → CARE PLAN → CONSENT / CAPACITY → STAFFING / QUALITY → MEDICATION / RESTRAINT → SAFEGUARDING → INSPECTION / COMPLAINT → TRANSFER / CLOSURE

```text
CARE NEED
＝physical? cognitive? psychosocial? ADL/IADL? temporary or long-term?

PERSON
＝older person? disabled adult? younger person with chronic need?

SETTING
＝home? community? day care? assisted living? nursing facility?

PROVIDER
＝public? nonprofit? private? individual worker? licensed institution?

FUNDING / ELIGIBILITY
＝tax-funded? insurance? means-tested? private pay? mixed?

ASSESSMENT
＝who assesses? objective criteria? reassessment?

CARE PLAN
＝goals? services? preferences? review?

CONSENT / CAPACITY
＝person decides? supported decision? representative? emergency?

STAFFING / QUALITY
＝ratio? qualification? continuity? training? infection control?

MEDICATION / RESTRAINT
＝authority? necessity? monitoring? least restrictive?

SAFEGUARDING
＝abuse? neglect? exploitation? reporting? protection?

INSPECTION / COMPLAINT
＝regulator? ombud? appeal? family complaint?

TRANSFER / CLOSURE
＝notice? continuity? records? resident relocation?
```

```yaml
  english_name: "Real Property / Land Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "土地與建物具有固定位置、稀缺性、長期性與多人同時可能主張使用／收益／擔保／占有利益的特性時，法律如何把一個物理空間切成可辨識、可公示、可移轉、可對抗第三人的權利結構？"

  historical_reason:
    "土地不像一般動產可以簡單交付就完成權利移轉。它與住所、生產、農業、城市規劃、道路、鄰地、抵押融資、繼承與公共建設長期連結，而且同一土地可以同時存在所有權、租賃、抵押、地役、占有與公共法限制。因此土地法發展出登記、公示、優先順位、用益、擔保、鄰地與土地利用規制等制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "所有權排他性 vs 他人使用／居住／通行利益"
    - "私人財產 vs 土地利用公共利益"
    - "登記公示 vs 真實權利狀態"
    - "交易安全 vs 個別實質公平"
    - "所有權自由 vs 鄰地關係"
    - "土地開發 vs 環境／文化／社區"
    - "財產權保障 vs 徵收／公共建設"
    - "抵押融資效率 vs 居住與債務人保護"

  shortest_maxim:
    "土地法第一眼不是『誰住在那裡』或『誰名字在登記簿』；先問『哪一塊不動產、哪一種權利、怎麼取得、是否公示、能不能對抗誰、還被什麼公法限制』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 25.1 第一眼｜PARCEL / UNIT → RIGHT → TITLE → POSSESSION → ENCUMBRANCE → PUBLIC LAW

```text
OBJECT
＝哪一筆土地？哪一棟建物？哪一個區分所有單位？界址在哪？

RIGHT
＝所有權？租賃？地上／用益權？地役？抵押？占有？共有？

TITLE / REGISTRATION
＝權利如何取得？是否需登記？登記內容是什麼？

POSSESSION
＝誰實際控制、使用？

ENCUMBRANCE / PRIORITY
＝有沒有抵押、查封、租賃、優先權、限制登記？

PUBLIC LAW
＝都市計畫、分區、建築、農地、文化、環境、徵收等限制？
```

核心：

> **土地是一個物理空間，但法律可以在同一空間上疊很多不同權利層。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "TENANCY_LAW_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__ENGLAND_RRA2025_CORE_PRIVATE_ASSURED_TENANCY_REFORM_COMMENCED_2026_05_01__LOCAL_RENT_DEPOSIT_HABITABILITY_EVICTION_RULES_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

Residential tenancy is jurisdiction-specific. In England, a 2026 commencement instrument brought core Renters’ Rights Act 2025 private assured-tenancy reforms into force on 1 May 2026, including abolition of section 21 no-fault eviction in that scope; other jurisdictions and local housing rules remain separate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UK legislation.gov.uk — Renters’ Rights Act 2025 Commencement No. 2 Regulations 2026**
  - URL: https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2026/421/contents/made
  - Verified point: Core private-sector assured-tenancy Chapter 1 reforms commenced on 1 May 2026 in England within the instrument’s stated scope.

依 INT-12 保留為 PROPERTY_POSITION_USE_GOVERNANCE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-187-REAL_PROPERTY_TENANCY_LANDLORD_TENANT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "不動產租賃／房東房客法"
  english_name: "Real Property Tenancy / Landlord-Tenant Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某人占有住宅、商業空間、房間或其他不動產時，該占有是lease / tenancy / licence / lodging / public housing / other status中的哪一種；租金、押金、修繕、適居、使用、房東進入、轉租讓與、租金調整、終止、possession與eviction各自需要什麼實體與程序資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "不動產租賃同時具有物權、契約與居住社會政策三種面向。房東仍是所有人，但把特定期間的占有與使用交給承租人後，所有權不能再被當成立即排除占有的自動執行權。住宅又與家庭生活、安全、無家可歸風險及結構性議價差距連結，因此多數法域對deposit、repair、notice、eviction、rent與anti-retaliation建立不同程度的強制規範；商業租賃則常給更大契約自治。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識premises、occupancy status與適用租賃制度，再固定租約、租期、租金／押金、condition、repair、entry、use與transfer；只有在termination ground、notice、程序與defence完成後，才進possession / eviction。不得從landlord title直接跳到立即收回房屋。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "ownership vs protected possession"
    - "contract freedom vs mandatory housing protection"
    - "rent predictability vs market adjustment"
    - "landlord access vs tenant privacy"
    - "maintenance cost vs habitability"
    - "security of tenure vs owner recovery"
    - "anti-social behaviour control vs eviction proportionality"
    - "commercial certainty vs residential vulnerability"
    - "deposit security vs landlord damage claims"
    - "tenant mobility vs fixed investment"
    - "private rental market vs housing policy"
    - "eviction efficiency vs due process / homelessness risk"

  shortest_maxim:
    "房東房客第一眼不是『房子是誰的』；先問『這個人憑什麼住、屬哪種tenancy、租約與強制法給雙方什麼權利、終止理由與通知是否成立、法院／行政程序走完了沒有』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-13.1 第一眼｜PREMISES → OCCUPANCY STATUS → REGIME → TERMS → RENT / DEPOSIT → CONDITION → ENTRY / USE → TRANSFER → TERMINATION → POSSESSION / EVICTION → REMEDY

```text
PREMISES
＝whole house? flat? room? commercial unit? mixed use?

OCCUPANCY STATUS
＝tenant? licensee? lodger? employee housing? social tenant?

REGIME
＝private residential? social? commercial? regulated?

TERMS
＝written? oral? fixed? periodic? statutory conversion?

RENT / DEPOSIT
＝amount? increase? index? protection? deductions?

CONDITION
＝repair? habitability? safety? utilities?

ENTRY / USE
＝privacy? inspection? quiet enjoyment? nuisance?

TRANSFER
＝assignment? sublease? succession?

TERMINATION
＝tenant notice? landlord ground? breach? sale? own use?

POSSESSION / EVICTION
＝notice? court order? enforcement? self-help prohibited?

REMEDY
＝repair order? rent reduction? damages? possession? penalty?
```

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-193-CONDOMINIUM_STRATA_HOMEOWNERS_ASSOCIATION_GOVERNANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公寓／區分所有／Strata／HOA治理法"
  english_name: "Condominium / Strata / Homeowners-Association Governance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一棟或一個社區被切成individual units / lots並保留common property後，誰因所有權自動成為共同治理體成員；規約／by-laws、owners meeting、management committee / owners corporation如何取得決策權；quorum、vote、assessment、common fund、repair、insurance、records、manager delegation、rule enforcement與爭議救濟如何限制『多數』對個別所有權人與住戶的權力？"

  historical_reason:
    "區分所有把同一建築同時切成私人專有權與不可完全分離的共同部分。每戶可以擁有自己的空間，卻無法單獨維護屋頂、電梯、結構、消防、外牆、公共基金與共同保險。因此法律必須在私人所有權上再建立一層共同治理。這個治理體既不是一般公司，也不是政府；它能收管理費、訂規約、維修共用部分、限制使用甚至採取訴訟，卻又必須受法定權限、程序、表決與少數權利限制。"

  first_move:
    "先固定scheme / building及unit-common-property boundary，再查membership、registered declaration / strata plan / condominium instrument與by-laws；之後逐項確認哪個body有權決定、meeting是否有效、vote門檻、money authority、maintenance duty與enforcement source。不得因『管委會決議』或『多數同意』就跳過權限與程序。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "individual ownership vs collective maintenance"
    - "majority governance vs minority rights"
    - "private rule-making vs statutory limits"
    - "community uniformity vs home autonomy"
    - "assessment collection vs financial accountability"
    - "common-property repair vs unit-owner responsibility"
    - "board efficiency vs owners-meeting authority"
    - "records transparency vs privacy"
    - "rule enforcement vs discrimination / proportionality"
    - "property value vs accessibility / reasonable accommodation"
    - "long-term capital maintenance vs present affordability"
    - "developer control vs owner control"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公寓治理第一眼不是『管委會說了算』；先問『哪一部分是誰的、哪個治理體依法存在、這件事是誰的權限、會議與表決門檻有沒有成立、錢與修繕責任怎麼分、決議是否碰到上位法與個別權利』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-36.1 第一眼｜SCHEME → UNIT / COMMON PROPERTY → MEMBERSHIP → INSTRUMENT / BY-LAWS → BODY → MEETING → VOTE → MONEY → MAINTENANCE → INSURANCE → RECORDS → ENFORCEMENT → DISPUTE

```text
SCHEME
＝condominium? strata? commonhold? HOA? planned community?

UNIT / COMMON PROPERTY
＝exclusive unit? common area? limited common property?

MEMBERSHIP
＝unit owner? tenant? occupant? developer? mortgagee?

INSTRUMENT / BY-LAWS
＝registered declaration? plan? rules? amendments?

BODY
＝owners meeting? board? committee? manager?

MEETING
＝notice? agenda? proxy? quorum?

VOTE
＝ordinary? special? unanimity? unit entitlement?

MONEY
＝assessment? reserve / sinking fund? special levy?

MAINTENANCE
＝unit? common property? source of damage?

INSURANCE
＝building? common liability? unit contents?

RECORDS
＝minutes? accounts? contracts? owner access?

ENFORCEMENT
＝notice? fine? injunction? compulsory sale where law allows?

DISPUTE
＝internal process? tribunal? court? regulator?
```

# DLM-100｜代理／代表法

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "AGENCY_REPRESENTATION_LAW_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__DIGITAL_AUTHORIZATION_AUTOMATED_SYSTEM_AND_APPARENT_AUTHORITY_RULES_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Agency and representation remain jurisdiction-specific. Digital authorization, automated contracting and AI-assisted acts may change evidence and attribution questions, but no transnational model law creates a universal law of authority or apparent authority.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — Model Law on Automated Contracting (2024)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/ecommerce/modellaw/automated_contracting
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL adopted the Model Law in 2024; it is recommended for States to consider when revising/adopting domestic law.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-100-AGENCY_REPRESENTATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "代理／代表法"
  english_name: "Agency / Representation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "principal的法律位置在什麼條件下可被agent / representative對third party的行為直接改變；authority從何產生、範圍多大、第三人能否合理依賴、超權／無權行為能否ratify、internal duties與external effects如何分開、termination何時對第三人生效？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代交易不可能全部由本人親自完成，公司、家庭、商業、金融與跨境交易都需要他人代為談判、簽約、收付款或傳達意思。代理制度讓法律效果跨越『行為者本人』而歸屬另一主體，但這同時創造嚴重風險：誰真的授權、第三人看見什麼、agent能否利用資訊優勢、principal能否事後否認。代理法因此圍繞authority、manifestation、reliance、conflict、accounting、termination與third-party protection建立一條可追溯的代表權鏈。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "principal autonomy vs third-party reliance"
    - "internal instruction vs external appearance"
    - "commercial speed vs verification of authority"
    - "agent discretion vs fiduciary / loyalty duties"
    - "delegation efficiency vs unauthorized substitution"
    - "undisclosed principal vs third-party expectations"
    - "termination freedom vs lingering apparent authority"
    - "corporate organs vs agency concepts"
    - "independent contractor status vs representative authority"
    - "digital / AI tools vs human / legal agency"
    - "choice of law vs mandatory protective rules"
    - "commission incentives vs conflicts"

  shortest_maxim:
    "代理法第一眼不是『他替公司說話』；先問『誰是principal、誰是agent、authority從哪裡來、第三人看見什麼、行為是否在scope內、超權後誰ratify／否認、終止是否已有效通知』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 106.1 第一眼｜PRINCIPAL → AGENT / REPRESENTATIVE → SOURCE OF AUTHORITY → SCOPE → THIRD-PARTY MANIFESTATION / KNOWLEDGE → ACT → ATTRIBUTION OF EFFECT → INTERNAL DUTIES → TERMINATION → RATIFICATION / REMEDY

```text
PRINCIPAL
＝whose legal position may change?

AGENT / REPRESENTATIVE
＝who acts / negotiates / communicates?

SOURCE OF AUTHORITY
＝contract? power of attorney? statute? office? apparent authority?

SCOPE
＝what transactions, amount, territory, duration?

THIRD-PARTY POSITION
＝what did third party know / reasonably rely on?

ACT
＝negotiation? signature? receipt? notice? payment?

ATTRIBUTION
＝is principal bound, agent bound, both, neither?

INTERNAL DUTIES
＝loyalty? care? accounting? instruction? commission?

TERMINATION
＝when did authority end internally / externally?

RATIFICATION / REMEDY
＝can unauthorized act be adopted? damages? restitution?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 106.31 Evidence architecture

- agency agreement；
- power of attorney；
- board / corporate resolution；
- registry；
- email / instructions；
- prior course of dealing；
- website / business card / title；
- third-party inquiry；
- commission records；
- conflict disclosure；
- signature / e-sign logs；
- revocation notice；
- public announcement；
- ratification conduct；
- third-party reliance evidence。

核心：

> **代理案真正要證明的是authority如何從principal跨到agent，再如何被第三人合理看見；最危險的捷徑是只看agent手上的名片、職稱或簽名。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "HCCH_TRUSTS_CONVENTION_CURRENT_FOR_PARTIES__NOT_GLOBAL_TRUST_CODE__DOMESTIC_TRUST_BENEFICIAL_OWNERSHIP_TAX_AML_AND_RECOGNITION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification and remedies not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The 1985 HCCH Trusts Convention remains in force for its Contracting Parties, but it is not a global trust code. Trust validity, beneficial ownership, AML/tax treatment, asset protection and recognition must be checked by jurisdiction and party status.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **HCCH — 1985 Trusts Convention status table**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=59
  - Verified point: Convention entered into force 1 January 1992 and has a limited Contracting-Party scope; it is not a universal trust code.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 AUTHORITY_FIDUCIARY_CONTROL 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-101-TRUSTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "信託法"
  english_name: "Trusts Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "settlor如何把特定assets置於trustee控制，使其形成與trustee私人財產分離、為beneficiary或purpose服務的trust fund；trust何時有效成立、由何law治理、trustee有哪些powers / duties、beneficiary如何監督、third-party / creditor / insolvency / succession如何處理、跨境trust如何recognize？"

  historical_reason:
    "trust把名義title、財產控制、利益享有與目的拘束拆成不同法律位置，使estate planning、charity、investment、pension、asset management與commercial structures可以長期運作。但同樣的分離也可能被用於隱藏ownership、規避creditors、tax或forced-heirship。信託法的核心因此不是『神秘資產盒子』，而是asset segregation、fiduciary accountability、beneficiary protection、third-party effects與mandatory-law boundaries。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "settlor autonomy vs mandatory family / creditor rules"
    - "trustee control vs beneficiary interests"
    - "asset segregation vs transparency"
    - "long-term purpose vs changing circumstances"
    - "fiduciary discretion vs accountability"
    - "confidentiality vs AML / tax / beneficial-ownership transparency"
    - "cross-border recognition vs domestic property categories"
    - "asset protection vs fraudulent transfer / insolvency"
    - "private trust vs charitable / purpose trust"
    - "trustee delegation vs non-delegable judgment"
    - "investment freedom vs prudence / diversification"
    - "trust law vs succession / matrimonial property"

  shortest_maxim:
    "信託法第一眼不是『資產放進信託所以不再屬於任何人』；先問『哪些資產、誰是settlor / trustee / beneficiary或purpose、資產是否真正進入trust fund、哪個law治理、trustee能做什麼、誰可追責、creditor / spouse / heir / tax law能否介入』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 107.1 第一眼｜SETTLOR → TRUST INTENTION / INSTRUMENT → TRUST PROPERTY → TRUSTEE → BENEFICIARY / PURPOSE → GOVERNING LAW → SEGREGATION → POWERS / DUTIES → ADMINISTRATION → THIRD PARTIES / CREDITORS → DISTRIBUTION / TERMINATION → RECOGNITION / REMEDY

```text
SETTLOR
＝who creates / contributes property?

TRUST INTENTION / INSTRUMENT
＝deed? will? declaration? court-created where law allows?

TRUST PROPERTY
＝what assets are actually subject to trust?

TRUSTEE
＝who holds / controls assets and owes duties?

BENEFICIARY / PURPOSE
＝for whom / what is trust administered?

GOVERNING LAW
＝choice? closest connection?

SEGREGATION
＝is property separate from trustee own estate?

POWERS / DUTIES
＝invest? distribute? delegate? account?

ADMINISTRATION
＝records? valuation? conflicts? tax?

THIRD PARTIES / CREDITORS
＝what can trustee creditors / buyers / secured creditors reach?

DISTRIBUTION / TERMINATION
＝when / how assets leave trust?

RECOGNITION / REMEDY
＝cross-border recognition? breach of trust? tracing?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 107.37 Evidence architecture

- trust deed / will；
- asset transfer documents；
- trustee acceptance；
- bank / custody account；
- registry；
- beneficiary schedule / class；
- protector powers；
- investment policy；
- trustee minutes；
- distributions；
- accounts；
- conflict disclosures；
- tax filings；
- creditor claims；
- settlor control evidence；
- third-party transfers；
- governing-law clause；
- HCCH party / reservation status。

核心：

> **信託案不能只拿trust deed；真正要證明的是資產是否進入、是否被分離管理、trustee是否真的以trustee身份行事，以及外部creditor / spouse / heir / purchaser能否依法介入。**

UNCITRAL adopted the Model Law on Automated Contracting in 2024. It is a model law recommended for enactment, not a self-executing global contract code; domestic formation, mandatory consumer and private-international-law rules remain decisive.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — Model Law on Automated Contracting (2024)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/ecommerce/modellaw/automated_contracting
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL adopted the Model Law in 2024; it is recommended for States to consider when revising/adopting domestic law.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-031-CONTRACT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "契約法"
  english_name: "Contract Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "私人承諾何時取得法律拘束力；法律如何辨識真正合意、決定契約內容、分配履約風險，並在違約、情事變更、無效、解除與第三人關係中維持交易可預測性？"

  historical_reason:
    "市場交換與私人合作需要讓可合理信賴的承諾具有穩定性，但若所有說過的話都具有完整法律強制力，日常社交與談判會被過度法律化；若所有契約文字都絕對執行，又可能放任詐欺、脅迫、重大資訊不對等與不公平條款。因此契約法透過成立、意思表示、解釋、效力、履行、違約與救濟規則，決定私人自治何時取得法律資格。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "契約自由 vs 強制法／公序"
    - "文字確定性 vs 真實共同意思"
    - "交易安全 vs 詐欺／錯誤／脅迫"
    - "承諾拘束力 vs 情事重大變更"
    - "形式自由 vs 重要交易形式要求"
    - "當事人自治 vs 弱勢保護"
    - "完整履行 vs 效率性違約／替代履行"
    - "個別契約 vs 第三人與市場外部性"
    - "私法自治 vs 公法監管"

  shortest_maxim:
    "契約法第一眼不是『有沒有一份合約』；先問『誰以什麼權限向誰作了什麼承諾、雙方真正同意了什麼、哪些內容依法有效、哪一項義務到期、誰以什麼方式違反』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 37.1 第一眼｜PARTY → AUTHORITY → FORMATION → TERMS → VALIDITY → PERFORMANCE → BREACH → REMEDY

契約案件先拆：

```text
PARTY
＝真正契約當事人是誰？

AUTHORITY
＝簽約人有無代表／代理權？

FORMATION
＝要約、承諾、對價／原因／合意等法域要件是否成立？

TERMS
＝契約內容究竟是什麼？

VALIDITY
＝有無無效、撤銷、詐欺、錯誤、脅迫、公序、強制法問題？

PERFORMANCE
＝各方何時、何地、以何標準履行？

BREACH
＝哪一項具體義務被違反？

REMEDY
＝履行、修補、解除、減價、賠償、返還？
```

核心：

```text
CONTRACT DOCUMENT EXISTS
≠ VALID CONTRACT
```

也：

```text
VALID CONTRACT
≠ EVERY CLAUSE ENFORCEABLE
```

UNCITRAL reports legislation based on or influenced by MLETR in 13 States / 13 jurisdictions. UNCITRAL expressly warns that enactment does not establish the legislation’s date of entry into force and that domestic deviations must be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — MLETR status**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/ecommerce/modellaw/electronic_transferable_records/status
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL reports enactments in 13 States / 13 jurisdictions and expressly distinguishes enactment from domestic entry into force.

依 INT-12 保留為 VOLUNTARY_TRANSACTION_EXCHANGE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-111-NEGOTIABLE_CARGO_DOCUMENTS_WAREHOUSE_RECEIPTS_ELECTRONIC_TRANSFERABLE_RECORDS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "可轉讓貨物單證／倉單與電子可轉讓紀錄法"
  english_name: "Negotiable Cargo Documents / Warehouse Receipts & Electronic Transferable Records Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "貨物在transport / storage期間不由交易人直接占有時，哪一種document / electronic record可以承載delivery、disposal、transfer、security與protected-holder effects；transport operator / warehouse operator簽發了什麼、holder如何取得、紙本possession如何轉成electronic control、遺失／偽造／重複簽發／medium conversion如何避免同一批貨物同時被兩個人主張？"

  historical_reason:
    "遠距貿易最困難的問題之一，是貨物在海上、鐵路、卡車、倉庫裡時，買賣與融資仍需要移轉控制。海運bill of lading長期具有document-of-title功能，但road/rail/air consignment notes通常不可轉讓；倉儲也需要用receipt讓存貨可融資。數位化又使紙本『唯一原件＋占有』失去物理基礎。因此現代統一法同時在做三件事：擴張跨運輸模式的negotiable cargo document、現代化warehouse receipts、以exclusive control替代paper possession。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "document control vs physical goods"
    - "negotiability vs true-owner protection"
    - "trade finance vs duplicate-document fraud"
    - "transport law vs document-of-title law"
    - "warehouse custody vs ownership"
    - "paper possession vs electronic control"
    - "technology neutrality vs reliability"
    - "electronic duplication vs singular authoritative record"
    - "holder protection vs forged transfer"
    - "security interests vs goods delivery"
    - "medium conversion vs double title"
    - "cross-border recognition vs domestic substantive law"
    - "new treaty design vs entry-into-force reality"

  shortest_maxim:
    "可轉讓單證法第一眼不是『誰拿著文件』；先問『貨是在運輸還是倉儲、哪種法源讓這張document/record代表delivery或goods rights、holder怎麼取得、電子情況下誰有exclusive control，以及同一批貨物是否可能被第二張單證重複主張』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 117.1 第一眼｜GOODS STATUS → DOCUMENT TYPE → ISSUER → SUBSTANTIVE REGIME → HOLDER / CONTROL → TRANSFER → DELIVERY / DISPOSAL RIGHT → SECURITY → LOSS / FORGERY / DUPLICATION → ELECTRONIC MEDIUM → THIRD PARTY / INSOLVENCY

```text
GOODS STATUS
＝in transit? in warehouse? already delivered?

DOCUMENT TYPE
＝bill of lading? consignment note? NCD? warehouse receipt?

ISSUER
＝carrier / transport operator? warehouse operator?

SUBSTANTIVE REGIME
＝maritime/transport law? NCD treaty? warehouse law?

HOLDER / CONTROL
＝paper possession? endorsement? electronic exclusive control?

TRANSFER
＝endorsement + delivery? control transfer?

DELIVERY / DISPOSAL RIGHT
＝who can instruct / demand delivery?

SECURITY
＝pledge/security right in document/goods?

LOSS / FORGERY / DUPLICATION
＝original missing? forged endorsement? double issue?

ELECTRONIC MEDIUM
＝MLETR-like law? reliable system? change of medium?

THIRD PARTY / INSOLVENCY
＝protected holder? competing creditor? operator insolvency?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 117.36 Evidence architecture

- transport/storage contract；
- original transport doc / receipt；
- NCD Convention reference；
- issuer agreement；
- endorsement chain；
- physical possession；
- e-record control log；
- issuance / cancellation；
- change-of-medium record；
- goods inventory；
- warehouse audit；
- carrier tracking；
- delivery instruction；
- security filing；
- competing holder；
- lost/forged notice；
- MLETR domestic statute；
- MLWR enactment；
- NCD treaty status / future ratification。

核心：

> **可轉讓貨物單證案要同時驗兩個世界：document chain是否完整，physical goods chain是否存在。任何只看其中一邊的系統，都可能得到一張法律上漂亮、現實裡卻沒有貨的單證。**


UNCITRAL’s electronic-commerce instruments remain current and the 2024 Model Law on Automated Contracting addresses automated/AI contracting, but model-law status is not domestic enactment. Form, signature, consumer, evidence and AI rules remain local.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — Electronic Commerce texts**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/ecommerce
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL lists MLEC, MLES, MLETR, identity/trust-services model law and the 2024 automated-contracting model law as separate instruments.
- **UNCITRAL — Model Law on Automated Contracting (2024)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/ecommerce/modellaw/automated_contracting
  - Verified point: UNCITRAL adopted the Model Law in 2024; it is recommended for States to consider when revising/adopting domestic law.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 VOLUNTARY_TRANSACTION_EXCHANGE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-112-ELECTRONIC_COMMERCE_ELECTRONIC_CONTRACTING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "電子商務／電子契約法"
  english_name: "Electronic Commerce / Electronic Contracting Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "電子message、website、app、platform、API、EDI或automated system所產生的communication，何時構成offer / acceptance / notice / amendment / performance；電子形式何時滿足writing、signature、original、retention與dispatch/receipt要求；錯誤、automation、cross-border scope、consumer law與mandatory formalities如何限制electronic equivalence？"

  historical_reason:
    "紙本交易依賴紙張、郵寄、親簽與物理原件。電子交易把這些功能拆成data messages、system logs、electronic signatures與automated messages。法律若只要求『紙』會排除新交易；若只說『網路上一律有效』又會犧牲形式、證據與保護功能。現代電子商務法因此以non-discrimination、technology neutrality與functional equivalence為核心，逐一問電子方式能否完成原本法律要求的功能。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "technology neutrality vs legal certainty"
    - "speed vs informed consent"
    - "automation vs attribution"
    - "click convenience vs dark patterns"
    - "cross-border trade vs mandatory domestic form"
    - "electronic availability vs durable retention"
    - "server timestamps vs legal receipt"
    - "machine-to-machine execution vs human responsibility"
    - "party autonomy vs consumer protection"
    - "code determinism vs legal remedies"
    - "electronic evidence abundance vs context integrity"
    - "paperless trade vs excluded transferable instruments"

  shortest_maxim:
    "電子商務法第一眼不是『有沒有按下同意』；先問『誰向誰送了什麼message、當時介面讓對方看見什麼、哪一刻法律視為發出／收到、哪個form requirement要被電子方式完成、系統行為最後歸屬給誰』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 118.1 第一眼｜PARTIES → INTERFACE / CHANNEL → MESSAGE → OFFER / INVITATION → ACCEPTANCE → FORM REQUIREMENT → TIME / PLACE → ATTRIBUTION → AUTOMATION → ERROR → RETENTION → REMEDY

```text
PARTIES
→ consumer? merchant? automated agent? platform?

INTERFACE / CHANNEL
→ email? website? app? API? EDI?

MESSAGE
→ quote? offer? order? confirmation? notice?

OFFER / INVITATION
→ binding intent or invitation to transact?

ACCEPTANCE
→ click? reply? performance? automated response?

FORM REQUIREMENT
→ writing? signature? original? notice?

TIME / PLACE
→ dispatch? receipt? business location?

ATTRIBUTION
→ whose system / credential?

AUTOMATION
→ rule engine? AI? smart contract?

ERROR
→ input error? system malfunction? pricing glitch?

RETENTION
→ durable, accessible record?

REMEDY
→ withdrawal? correction? rescission? damages?
```

- **European Commission — Democracy implementation tracker**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/priorities-2024-2029/democracy-and-our-values/implementation-tracker_en

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 VOLUNTARY_TRANSACTION_EXCHANGE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-180-STANDARD_FORM_CONTRACTS_UNFAIR_TERMS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "定型化契約／不公平條款法"
  english_name: "Standard-Form Contracts / Unfair Terms Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一方預先擬定、反覆使用且另一方通常缺乏實質談判能力的條款，何時有效進入契約；其文字、呈現、透明度、核心價格／給付、免責、單方變更、違約金、續約、爭端條款與其他風險配置，何時因不公平、顯失平衡、違反誠信或強制法而不拘束、失效或受到集體／行政控制？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代市場依靠標準條款降低大量交易成本，但同一效率也讓起草者可以把風險一次性轉嫁給成千上萬的相對人。單純說『有簽名』『有按同意』無法回答條款是否曾被看見、是否真正納入、是否透明、是否超出合理期待、是否違反強制保護。消費者法最明顯，但保險、銀行、平台、運輸與B2B條款也可能有各自特殊控制。"

  first_move:
    "先問 term 是否被有效 incorporation，而不是先問 unfairness；接著確認是否 individually negotiated、相對人身分、透明度與適用的 mandatory regime，再分析 substantive imbalance / exclusion / unilateral power / remedy restriction。最後才決定不拘束、部分無效、整體存續、解釋或集體禁用等效果。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "transaction efficiency vs bargaining asymmetry"
    - "freedom of contract vs mandatory fairness"
    - "signature / click certainty vs meaningful notice"
    - "plain language vs substantive imbalance"
    - "low price vs hidden legal risk"
    - "individual consent vs mass-form architecture"
    - "core terms vs ancillary terms"
    - "business autonomy vs consumer protection"
    - "severance vs collapse of contract"
    - "individual litigation vs collective prevention"
    - "cross-border choice of law vs mandatory protection"

  shortest_maxim:
    "定型化條款第一眼不是『他有沒有簽』；先問『這條款有沒有真的進契約、誰擬的、能不能談、是否清楚、它改變了哪個法定基線、失衡到什麼程度、法律允不允許它繼續拘束』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-201.1 第一眼｜STATUS → STANDARD TERM → NOTICE / INCORPORATION → NEGOTIATION → TRANSPARENCY → SUBSTANCE → MANDATORY LAW → EFFECT → COLLECTIVE CONTROL

```text
STATUS
＝consumer? business? employee? insured? platform user?

STANDARD TERM
＝drafted in advance? repeated use? one-sided authorship?

NOTICE / INCORPORATION
＝provided when? accessible? unusual term highlighted?

NEGOTIATION
＝could counterparty influence substance?

TRANSPARENCY
＝plain? intelligible? economic consequence understandable?

SUBSTANCE
＝exclusion? unilateral change? penalty? arbitration? renewal? price?

MANDATORY LAW
＝consumer? insurance? employment? banking? sector rule?

EFFECT
＝not binding? void? severed? interpreted narrowly? contract continues?

COLLECTIVE CONTROL
＝injunction? regulator? representative action?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-201.13 Evidence architecture

- signed agreement；
- terms version；
- checkout screenshot；
- click log；
- hyperlink placement；
- notice email；
- change notice；
- negotiation correspondence；
- draft history；
- pricing page；
- fee table；
- renewal screen；
- cancellation flow；
- arbitration / forum term；
- translation；
- regulator guidance；
- black / grey list；
- sector rules；
- representative action / injunction records。

核心：

> **定型化契約不是「大量簽名」取得正當性；大量使用反而要求更精確地審查一個條款如何被納入、被理解、分配風險與反覆影響他人。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_SUCCESSION_REGULATION_CURRENT_FOR_PARTICIPATING_SCOPE__DEATH_CUTOVER_2015_08_17__DIGITAL_ASSET_WILL_FORCED_HEIRSHIP_AND_DOMESTIC_RULES_REQUIRED"
```

EU Regulation 650/2012 remains the succession framework in its participating scope and applies to succession of persons dying on or after 17 August 2015. Digital assets, wills, forced heirship, tax and domestic estate administration still require jurisdiction-specific law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Regulation (EU) No 650/2012**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=CELEX:32012R0650
  - Verified point: The Regulation applies to succession of persons who die on or after 17 August 2015 within its participating EU scope.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 GLOBAL REBASE，繼承法主住所由 E 移至 F：第一主軸是死亡後遺產、債務與財產承接；家庭身分仍保留為 cross-subject，不因搬家刪除來源卡。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-039-SUCCESSION_INHERITANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "繼承法"
  english_name: "Succession / Inheritance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "自然人死亡後，法律如何辨識遺產範圍、清償債務、確認遺囑效力、決定法定／遺囑繼承人與份額、保護特定家屬，並讓財產、企業與義務有秩序地移轉？"

  historical_reason:
    "死亡會終止自然人的人格，但其財產、契約、債務、企業、數位資產與家庭關係不能同時消失。若沒有繼承制度，財產歸屬與債務清償會陷入真空。繼承法因此建立遺產管理、遺囑自治、法定繼承、特留／強制份、債權人保護與遺產分割等制度，在死者意志、家庭保障、交易安全與債務清償之間分配利益。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "遺囑自由 vs 家屬最低保障"
    - "死者意思 vs 遺囑形式／能力"
    - "繼承人期待 vs 遺產債權人"
    - "家庭公平 vs 死者私人自治"
    - "快速承接財產 vs 清查債務"
    - "企業連續性 vs 多名繼承人分割"
    - "形式確定性 vs 疑似詐欺／脅迫"
    - "傳統繼承角色 vs 性別平等"
    - "實體資產 vs 數位資產"
    - "國內遺產 vs 跨境不動產／金融資產"

  shortest_maxim:
    "繼承法第一眼不是『誰是家人』；先問『誰死亡、留下哪些資產與債務、有沒有有效遺囑、哪些人依法取得什麼資格、誰先清償、剩下什麼才真正能分』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 45.1 第一眼｜DEATH → ESTATE → DEBTS → WILL → CAPACITY / FORM → HEIRS → PROTECTED SHARES → ADMINISTRATION → DISTRIBUTION

```text
DEATH
＝死亡何時法律上確定？

ESTATE
＝哪些資產屬死者？

DEBTS
＝債務、稅、費用、擔保？

WILL
＝有無遺囑？哪一版？

CAPACITY / FORM
＝遺囑能力、形式、見證、撤回？

HEIRS / BENEFICIARIES
＝法定繼承人／受遺贈人？

PROTECTED SHARES
＝有無特留分／forced heirship？

ADMINISTRATION
＝誰管理、清冊、變價、處理債權？

DISTRIBUTION
＝剩餘財產何時、如何分？
```

核心：

```text
DEATH
≠ ASSETS IMMEDIATELY FREE FOR HEIRS TO TAKE
```

核心：

> **繼承案件不是先分錢；先把死者的法律與財產位置做完清冊。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-032-TORT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "侵權法"
  english_name: "Tort Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "即使當事人沒有事先約定，人在社會中從事活動時，何時因製造、控制或不合理增加他人風險，而產生對他人身體、財產、人格或其他法律利益的損害責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "很多損害不是契約關係產生：車禍、產品事故、醫療傷害、環境污染、誹謗、危險活動等。若所有人只對自己簽過契約的人負責，社會中的外部風險會被大量轉嫁。因此侵權法透過注意義務、故意侵害、無過失責任、因果、損害與抗辯等制度，分配日常活動造成他人損失的責任。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "行動自由 vs 他人安全"
    - "過失責任 vs 無過失風險分配"
    - "個人責任 vs 企業／雇主責任"
    - "實際因果 vs 法律責任範圍"
    - "受害者補償 vs 過度責任"
    - "風險預防 vs 創新與社會活動"
    - "言論自由 vs 名譽／人格"
    - "私人自治 vs 公共安全"
    - "保險分散風險 vs 行為誘因"

  shortest_maxim:
    "侵權法第一眼不是『誰受傷就找誰賠』；先問『誰對誰負什麼法定注意／不得侵害義務、哪個行為違反它、風險如何變成這個損害、責任範圍應到哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 38.1 第一眼｜INTEREST → DUTY → CONDUCT → BREACH → FACTUAL CAUSE → LEGAL SCOPE → DAMAGE → DEFENCE

```text
INTEREST
＝生命？身體？財產？名譽？隱私？經濟利益？

DUTY
＝被告對原告有什麼法律義務？

CONDUCT
＝做了／沒做什麼？

BREACH
＝是否低於法定／合理注意標準？

FACTUAL CAUSE
＝沒有該行為，損害是否仍發生？

LEGAL SCOPE
＝該損害是否落在此義務所要防止的風險範圍？

DAMAGE
＝實際可賠損失？

DEFENCE
＝同意、與有過失、assumption of risk、免責等？
```

核心：

```text
HARM
≠ TORT LIABILITY
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 38.17 證據特徵

- incident record；
- CCTV；
- photos；
- medical records；
- repair estimates；
- expert report；
- safety standard；
- SOP；
- risk assessment；
- maintenance；
- warning；
- product sample；
- design document；
- employment record；
- witness；
- financial loss records；
- accident reconstruction；
- toxicology／epidemiology（如適用）。

核心：

> **侵權案件不是只證明「有人受傷」，而是要證明「這個被告的義務如何連到這個損害」。**

## 38.19 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域現查：

1. negligence；
2. duty of care；
3. professional liability；
4. omissions；
5. causation；
6. remoteness / scope of liability；

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 NONCONSENSUAL_OBLIGATION_ADJUSTMENT 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-178-UNJUST_ENRICHMENT_RESTITUTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "不當得利／返還法"
  english_name: "Unjust Enrichment / Restitution Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一方取得金錢、財產、服務、使用利益、債務免除或其他經濟利益時，何時因欠缺可支持其保有利益的法律基礎，而對另一方負返還、價額償還或其他 restitutionary obligation；返還範圍、受益消滅、善惡意、change of position、第三人與既有契約／侵權關係又如何限制請求？"

  historical_reason:
    "私人法若只有契約與侵權，仍會留下大量『沒有有效承諾、也未必有侵權，但一方保有另一方移轉利益缺乏法律基礎』的案件，例如誤付款、契約無效後已履行、預期基礎失敗、錯誤履行與無權取得。不同法系用 condictio、enrichissement injustifié、unjust enrichment、restitution 等不同結構處理。它們並非完全相同，但共同提醒：返還不是懲罰，也不是以結果不公平替代法律基礎分析。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識 claimed benefit 與 defendant 是否真正取得或保有，再找 transfer / enrichment 的法律基礎；若契約、法定義務、贈與或其他合法原因仍存在，不得只因原告後悔就改走不當得利。若基礎欠缺，再依適用法檢查 at-the-expense-of nexus、subsidiarity、defences、第三人與返還量。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "reversal of unjustified gain vs transactional finality"
    - "claimant loss vs defendant gain"
    - "restitution in kind vs monetary value"
    - "objective absence of basis vs subjective unfairness"
    - "mistake protection vs payment finality"
    - "failed contract vs contractual risk allocation"
    - "good-faith recipient protection vs claimant recovery"
    - "change of position vs full reversal"
    - "direct enrichment vs third-party transfers"
    - "restitution vs damages"
    - "proprietary recovery vs personal claim"

  shortest_maxim:
    "不當得利第一眼不是『對方拿到我的錢』；先問『他取得了什麼利益、從哪個法律關係而來、原本有沒有保有基礎、現在基礎是否失效、誰因此受損、返還到什麼程度才是在撤回不當利益而不是賠償所有損害』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-199.1 第一眼｜BENEFIT → RECEIPT / RETENTION → SOURCE → LEGAL BASIS → NEXUS → SUBSIDIARITY → DEFENCE → MEASURE → RESTITUTION

```text
BENEFIT
＝money? property? services? use? saved expenditure? discharged debt?

RECEIPT / RETENTION
＝did defendant actually obtain / retain it?

SOURCE
＝payment? transfer? invalid contract? failed purpose? mistake? third party?

LEGAL BASIS
＝contract? statute? judgment? gift? valid obligation?

NEXUS
＝at claimant's expense / legally connected how?

SUBSIDIARITY
＝is a contract / tort / statutory route controlling or excluding?

DEFENCE
＝change of position? good faith? limitation? passing on? illegality?

MEASURE
＝gain? claimant impoverishment? lesser amount? value received?

RESTITUTION
＝return in kind? money? accounting? other remedy?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-199.11 Evidence architecture

- payment records；
- bank transfer；
- invoice；
- contract / invalidity decision；
- purpose / condition communications；
- gift / donation evidence；
- recipient account；
- use / onward transfer；
- valuation evidence；
- services evidence；
- benefit acceptance；
- third-party transfer；
- insolvency records；
- choice-of-law facts；
- limitation timeline。

核心：

> **不當得利不是「有人得到好處」；是法律必須先失去一個足以支持保有該利益的基礎。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_PRODUCT_LIABILITY_2024_DIRECTIVE_IN_FORCE__TRANSPOSITION_DUE_2026_12_09__NEW_SCOPE_PRODUCTS_AFTER_2026_12_08__DOMESTIC_IMPLEMENTATION_REQUIRED"

  global_closure: false
```

Directive (EU) 2024/2853 is in force, but transposition is due 9 December 2026. A 7 May 2026 corrigendum corrected Article 2(1): the new Directive applies to products placed on the market or put into service after 8 December 2026.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **EUR-Lex — Directive (EU) 2024/2853**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/2853/oj
  - Verified point: Member States must transpose by 9 December 2026.
- **EUR-Lex — Corrigendum to Directive (EU) 2024/2853, 7 May 2026**
  - URL: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/2853/corrigendum/2026-05-07/oj
  - Verified point: Article 2(1) was corrected so the Directive applies to products placed on the market or put into service after 8 December 2026.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 NONCONSENSUAL_OBLIGATION_ADJUSTMENT 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-179-PRODUCT_LIABILITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "產品責任法"
  english_name: "Product Liability Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "產品、軟體或法定納入之相關數位組件造成生命、身體、財產或其他法定損害後，誰是可被追究的 economic operator；產品是否具有法律上的 defect，該 defect 是否造成本案 damage，舉證、推定、抗辯、時效與多方責任如何成立？"

  historical_reason:
    "大量生產把設計與製造風險分散到消費者難以觀察的供應鏈。事故發生後，僅靠一般過失常迫使受害人證明企業內部控制與技術決策。產品責任制度因此在不同法域透過 strict / no-fault liability、defect categories、供應鏈主體責任與證據推定重新分配風險。數位產品、AI、software updates與平台交易又使『產品』『製造者』『控制期間』與證明複雜性重新成為核心問題。"

  first_move:
    "先固定涉案 product / component、上市或投入使用時間與適用制度；再畫出 manufacturer / importer / distributor / platform / modifier 等供應鏈，分開證明 defect、damage、causation、法定推定與 defence。不得因產品出事或違反安全規則就直接完成民事責任。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "victim compensation vs innovation"
    - "strict liability vs causal proof"
    - "consumer information asymmetry vs defendant due process"
    - "product defect vs ordinary product risk"
    - "regulatory compliance vs civil defectiveness"
    - "physical products vs software / AI"
    - "original manufacture vs post-market modification"
    - "supply-chain complexity vs effective defendant"
    - "technical complexity vs evidence access"
    - "development risk vs victim protection"
    - "product safety prevention vs ex post compensation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "產品責任第一眼不是『產品害到人所以廠商賠』；先問『哪一個產品、哪一版／哪個時間、誰控制、哪裡有 defect、造成什麼法定 damage、因果怎麼接、哪個責任制度與推定真正適用』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-200.1 第一眼｜PRODUCT → TIME → ECONOMIC OPERATOR → DEFECT → DAMAGE → CAUSATION → EVIDENCE / PRESUMPTION → DEFENCE → LIMITATION → REMEDY

```text
PRODUCT
＝physical good? component? software? digital manufacturing file?

TIME
＝placed on market / put into service when?

ECONOMIC OPERATOR
＝manufacturer? component maker? importer? distributor? platform? modifier?

DEFECT
＝design? manufacture? warning / instruction? software/update? cybersecurity?

DAMAGE
＝death? personal injury? property? legally covered data loss?

CAUSATION
＝defect → damage?

EVIDENCE / PRESUMPTION
＝disclosure? mandatory safety violation? technical complexity?

DEFENCE
＝defect absent at market time? legal compliance? development risk? misuse?

LIMITATION
＝knowledge period? long-stop?

REMEDY
＝compensation / contribution / recourse?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-200.12 Evidence architecture

- product model / serial / version；
- purchase / supply record；
- placing-on-market date；
- software / firmware version；
- update history；
- product manual / warning；
- design / testing evidence；
- safety standard；
- recall / incident report；
- regulator findings；
- failed component；
- expert analysis；
- medical / property damage evidence；
- digital logs；
- economic-operator chain；
- importer / distributor records；
- platform listing；
- modification records；
- disclosure orders；
- causation expert report。

核心：

> **產品責任不是把「危險」翻成「賠償」；要把產品、時間、缺陷、責任主體、損害與因果重新接成一條民事資格鏈。**

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:

  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B11"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、C:金錢／資本／信用／風險流"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-033-DAMAGES_COMPENSATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "損害賠償法"
  english_name: "Damages / Compensation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當契約、侵權、法定責任或其他請求權已成立後，法律究竟要把受害者放回哪一個假設位置；哪些損失屬於該責任所造成、可以證明、可以預見／歸責，並應如何避免不足賠償、過度賠償與重複受償？"

  historical_reason:
    "責任成立只回答『誰應負責』，不回答『應負責多少』。實際損害可能包含過去與未來、財產與非財產、直接與間接、可精確計算與高度不確定項目。若沒有獨立的損害法理，裁判容易把情緒、責任嚴重性與金額混在一起。因此損害賠償法透過補償目標、因果範圍、可預見性、減損、折現、證明標準與禁止重複受償等規則，把責任轉化成可執行數額。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "完全賠償 vs 過度賠償"
    - "個別真實損失 vs 計算可行性"
    - "未來損失不確定 vs 不讓受害者承擔全部未知"
    - "精神／人格損害 vs 金錢不可比性"
    - "責任嚴重性 vs 補償性原則"
    - "損失證明要求 vs 被告造成的不確定"
    - "減損義務 vs 不苛責受害者"
    - "附帶利益 vs 禁止雙重受償"
    - "一次性賠償 vs 長期持續損失"

  shortest_maxim:
    "損害賠償第一眼不是『對方有錯所以要賠很多』；先問『要回復哪個法律利益、如果違法沒有發生世界會長什麼樣、哪些差額由本案造成、能證明到什麼程度、是否已被其他來源補償』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 39.1 第一眼｜LIABILITY BASIS → PROTECTED INTEREST → COUNTERFACTUAL → LOSS HEAD → CAUSATION → LIMITATION → MITIGATION → QUANTUM

```text
LIABILITY BASIS
＝違約？侵權？法定責任？其他？

PROTECTED INTEREST
＝履行利益？信賴？身體完整？財產？人格？

COUNTERFACTUAL
＝若違法／違約未發生，原告本來會處於什麼位置？

LOSS HEAD
＝醫療費？修復？營業損失？失能？精神？未來所得？

CAUSATION
＝該損失是否由本案行為造成？

LIMITATION
＝可預見性、remoteness、scope？

MITIGATION
＝是否合理減少損失？

QUANTUM
＝如何量化？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 39.22 證據特徵

- medical bills；
- payroll；
- tax returns；
- financial statements；
- invoices；
- repair estimates；
- replacement contracts；
- sales data；
- expert reports；
- actuarial tables；
- life expectancy；
- care plan；
- discount rate evidence；
- market benchmark；
- insurance payment；
- social benefit；
- mitigation records；
- job search；
- substitute transaction；
- interest calculation。

核心：

> **損害法最怕「責任很清楚，所以金額隨便估」；責任越清楚，越應把金額證據獨立做好。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置、地方制度、最新法規或動態名單，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 39.24 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與責任類型現查：

1. compensatory principle；
2. expectation / reliance / restitution；
3. consequential losses；
4. foreseeability / remoteness；
5. mitigation；
6. contributory negligence；
7. future loss；
8. loss of earnings；
9. lost profits；
10. loss of chance；
11. non-pecuniary loss；
12. death / dependency claims；
13. statutory caps；
14. punitive / exemplary damages；
15. liquidated damages / penalty；
16. collateral source；
17. subrogation；
18. double recovery；
19. pre/post-judgment interest；
20. tax treatment；
21. inflation / discounting；
22. limitation periods；
23. damage proof standard。

## FAILURE_MODES

## 39.23 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "被告很壞＝損害一定高。"
  - "營業額下降＝同額利潤損失。"
  - "有因果＝所有後續損失都可賠。"
  - "損失難算＝不能賠。"
  - "損失可能存在＝原告喊多少都可。"
  - "原告有保險＝被告一定少賠。"
  - "多個請求權＝同一損失可以重複受償。"
  - "違約金寫在契約＝一定全額有效。"
  - "精神痛苦嚴重＝金額沒有任何法律限制。"
  - "模型有精確數字＝計算已被證明。"
```

| `EU_EQUALITY_1500` | EUR-Lex — Directive (EU) 2024/1500 — https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/dir/2024/1500/oj | Transposition required by 19 June 2026; the Directive is in force and Article 23 applies from that date. |

```text
27 REFRESH_REQUIRED
→ 27 QUALIFIED CURRENT-LAW STATUS

但：

QUALIFIED STATUS PASS
!= EVERY JURISDICTION VERIFIED
!= TRANSPOSITION DEADLINE == COMPLETE IMPLEMENTATION
!= ENTRY INTO FORCE == FULL APPLICATION
!= MODEL LAW / RECOMMENDATION == DOMESTIC OPERATIVE LAW
!= TREATY EXISTS == THIS STATE IS BOUND IN THIS CASE
```

DB3 現在取得：

```text
V1.0 DB3 RELEASE-CANDIDATE QUALIFICATION
FOR STABLE METHOD + CURRENT FRAMEWORK / TRANSITION ROUTING

具體案件仍須重新查：
JURISDICTION / DATE / STATUS / RELATIONSHIP / CAPACITY / EMPLOYMENT CLASSIFICATION / FAMILY STATUS / PROPERTY TITLE / FORM / IMPLEMENTATION / REMEDY
```
